The evasive roll is 4 startup / 12 travel / 10 recovery (its travel and the knockdown roll get-up run at `UniversalMovementRules.RollSpeedMultiplier` **1.875** × run speed since 2026-10-12, 1.5 before, so both kept their distance through the speed cut below); the first eight roll-travel frames are invulnerable and roll travel disables the combatant pushbox. **Air dodge (A03, Package 13 W1, both modes):** Roll while airborne is an `AirDodge` sub-phase of `Rolling` — 4 startup / 8 invulnerable / 10 recovery, no added speed (the held direction shifts at most 1.0 unit), pushbox off while invulnerable, **once per airtime** (refreshed by landing, a ledge grab, a stock loss and, since 2026-10-16, any applied hitstun in both modes — decision 5.3, `USER-AIR-DODGE-REFRESH-ON-HIT`; a blocked, absorbed, armoured or Defy-zeroed hit applies none), legal from `Airborne` and an aerial swing's recovery/chain window, refused in hitstun (a tumble included while its hitstun runs)/daze/hitstop/grab/ledge hang/Ultimate/**Root** — since 2026-10-16 it is legal from a post-hitstun tumble, which it ends (decision 3.2; the M05/M07 bullet under Combat); landing does not cancel its recovery. Sim universal-movement phases 5–7, `AirDodgeUsed` on component 320; its invulnerability rides `InvulnerabilityFrames`, so it avoids an Ultimate activation strike. These timings live in `UniversalMovementRules` and must remain aligned between Story and Fighter simulation. **The universal dash was removed on 2026-08-09 by explicit user decision, superseding `design-godot.md`'s 12-frame `1.35x` dash spec** — the roll and each character's movement ability are the mobility tools. `CharacterState.Dashing`, `UniversalMovementPhase.Dash`, and the protocol-v2 Dash button bit remain reserved (never reuse them); enemy `ChargeDash` archetypes and character abilities that dash by name are unrelated and stay.
- Movement also includes direction-reversal skid, coyote time, jump buffering, short-hop behavior, air control, crouching, one-way platform drop-through, ledge hanging, and recovery. **Every character has a double jump as of 2026-08-10** (`MaxJumpCount` is 2 across the roster; run speeds −15% and jump forces −20% height in the same batch — the nine `.tres` values are law). **Since 2026-10-12 (user request 4, ledger `USER-MOVEMENT-SPEED-CUT`, both modes; `PROVISIONAL-SPEED-CUT-RULES`)** the nine `MaxMoveSpeed` values are × 0.8 again, exactly: cleopatra 5.8, einstein 5.4, joan 6.2, leonardo 5.2, lincoln 3.8, mozart 5.4, shakespeare 5.2, tesla 4.8, tubman 5.4 (Story Lincoln 228 px/s, Joan 372). Jump height and airtime are unchanged and every jump's distance falls exactly 20 %, because the air speed cap is the run speed (Lincoln's Story single jump 201 px, double 353 px); the roll keeps its distance (above); `FighterLoadout.Default.MoveSpeed` stays 7; absolute kit motions, knockback and every enemy speed are unchanged; `Level14Controller.ReferenceWalkSpeed` is 228, the Level 6 and 15 escape fronts 224 and 216 px/s, and Level 11's railcut perch widened to 340 px; every Fighter hash moved. Suites: `StoryMovementSpeedCutTests`, `FighterMovementSpeedCutTests`. On 2026-08-15 the `MaxJumpForce` spread was compressed from 10.0–13.5 to **11.5–13.0** by user direction (heavies jumped too low; ordering preserved, Lincoln 11.5 → Pocahontas 13.0 — since the Tubman swap the top is Cleopatra 12.75, Tubman 12.5; `docs/GAMEPLAY_FEEL_2026-08-10_PLAN.md` §2.2 has the per-character map). The Fighter sim also ledge-grabs at authored platform ends (`FighterLedgeRules`, plan §2.11: capture falling or rising slowly into a 0.5-unit edge box, hang refills jumps, Jump climbs at 0.9× jump speed, Down releases with a 30-frame regrab lockout, 300-frame auto-release, hits knock the hang off); Story keeps its marker-based `LedgeGrabPoint` system, now also capturing while rising slowly. **Since 2026-10-16 the Fighter hang also offers get-ups** (decision 5.2, `USER-FIGHTER-LEDGE-GETUPS`, `PROVISIONAL-LEDGE-GETUP-FRAMES`; Fighter only — Story's `LedgeGrabPoint` comes "later"), one action a tick, the climb jump winning its tick: a **neutral climb** on a fresh toward-stage or Up tilt (|stick| > 30) once the hang is `LedgeGetUpMinHangFrames` 6 old and the stick has re-armed (neutral, away or down on any hang tick; 323 `ActionFlags` bit 2 `LedgeStickRearmed`), 20 frames, intangible for 12; a **ledge roll** (Roll without Block or the Echo Step bit), 8 intangible climb frames, then the ordinary roll toward the stage; a **ledge attack** (BasicAttack without Block or the Grab bit), 8 intangible climb frames, then an 8 / 4 / 14 strike with string hit 1's box and knockback, 1.0 × damage, 30-frame hitstun and no launch, which hands the action chokepoint a `Swing`. Each climb moves the fighter to the stand position 0.5 u inside the edge (`FighterStageGeometry.TryGetStandPosition`), on 305's universal-movement values 8–10 (`LedgeClimb`, `LedgeRoll`, `LedgeAttackClimb`) and `AttackFlags` 512 `FlagLedgeAttack`; a hit ends it (`FighterLedgeRules`, `FighterCombatSystem.LedgeAttack.cs`; the numbers live in `BasicComboRules`' ledge section). The first grab of an airtime is intangible for `LedgeFirstGrabIntangibleFrames` 30, ended at every hang exit (`FighterLedgeRules.EndFirstGrabIntangibility`, CM-FIXB-SIM B8 / B19), so the climb jump and every get-up keep their own vulnerable frames; a regrab gets none. The CPU leaves a ledge only by the climb jump (`fa18442`: `FighterCpuController.Sample` sends a hanging CPU no stick, Roll or BasicAttack unless it is in hitstun or daze; `VERIFY-CPU-LEDGE-GETUPS`, closed). The Move List shows the get-ups in a Fighter-mode `movelist_ledge_options` row. **Ledge V7.3 (2026-08-26), both modes:** the edge is contested, not shared — a second grabber trumps the hanger (an end-of-update resolution pass in `FighterMovementSystem` releases the earlier hanger — larger `LedgeStateFrames`, lower PlayerID keeps a same-frame tie — via the normal release path with the 30-frame regrab lockout; Story's `LedgeGrabPoint.TryAcquire` forces the occupant off the same way), and grabs are capped at `BasicComboRules.LedgeRegrabsPerAirtime` (3) per airtime via `FighterVerbComponent.LedgeGrabsThisAirtime` (Story mirrors on `PlayerController`), reset on grounding and stock loss.
- Story Mode may use native Godot `CharacterBody2D` physics. Local Test Arena Fighter Mode already uses Klotho fixed-point authority and must remain on that boundary; online work must not move authority back into `PhysicsServer2D`.

### Combat

- Hit detection is `Area2D` hitboxes against `Area2D` hurtboxes. Activation timing comes from authored frame windows on the attacker's **physics frame clock** — never from the sprite's animation clock, animation callbacks or broad body collisions. Since the 2026-10-04 feel pass (F2, ledger `F2-SINGLE-FRAME-CLOCK`, superseding `design-godot.md`'s "AnimatedSprite2D frame callbacks" wording) every Story swing, template-timed or authored-profile, runs on `PlayerController`'s physics frame clock with its hitbox windows from `BasicComboRules` (the template or the character's string profile), every Special and Ultimate on `BaseSpecial`'s phase clock from its `AbilityData` frames, and the Fighter sim on its phase machine — the one clock hitstop suspends. Sprites are posed from those windows (`FTT.Combat.AttackPoseRules`).
- The basic combo has three hits using `0.8×`, `1.0×`, and `1.5×` `CharacterData.BasicAttackDamage` (the authored base damages were halved and Special1/Special2 `BaseDamage` doubled in the 2026-08-10 gameplay-feel batch; ultimates and movement abilities unchanged). Crouched and grounded-down attack inputs use the same three-hit string. **Two directional single strikes exist alongside the chain (2026-08-10):** Up held + attack is an upward launcher (grounded or airborne), and Down held + attack while airborne is a downward-hitting launcher (down-air — **since Package 13 W1 (A12) a straight-down spike** with ±15° DI: a slammed victim on or reaching floor/one-way ground bounces once, forced and untechable, into tumble with the Up-Attack vertical profile and a fresh DI read, and the next landing techs normally; `SlamBouncePending`/`SlamBounceSpeed` on component 320, and Story mobs bounce too); both use `BasicComboRules` frame data (7/8/18 and 6/10/16), 1.0× damage, 30-frame hitstun, mostly-vertical launch knockback, never chain, and reset the combo index. Selection is the shared pure helper `BasicComboRules.SelectAttackVariant`. **Since 2026-10-12 (user request 1, ledger `USER-AERIAL-SINGLE-STRIKE`, both modes; `PROVISIONAL-AERIAL-STRIKE-RULES`) the three-hit string is grounded only, and an airborne neutral press is the air strike** (`BasicComboRules.VariantAirAttack` = 3; the sim's `FighterBasicAttackRules.FlagAirAttack` = 128, a spare bit of 305's `AttackFlags`), a third single strike beside the Up-Attack and the Down-Air: the profile's air startup (Joan 4; template, Cleopatra, Leonardo, Mozart, Tesla, Tubman 5; Einstein, Shakespeare 6; Lincoln 7) / 7 / 13 frames, 1.0 × `BasicAttackDamage`, 30-frame hitstun, string hit 1's knockback, no launch, Story hitbox ID `air_attack` (in `IsBasicStringHitbox`, so it gets the D1 confirm, per-hit Momentum 20 and S4's ×1.25), pose `basic_attack_1`; its Story box is hit 1's x-span with a −64 … +40 px vertical span about the feet (`PlayerController.ResolveAirAttackBoxPixels`), the sim's `ResolveStringBox(0)`. A press during it is dropped (no buffer, queue or hold), it never chains, and **no chain hit starts in the air**: a held or buffered continuation that would begin airborne ends the string, while an airborne press in the chain hold is an air press. The selection is Up → Up-Attack; Down and airborne → Down-Air; airborne → the air strike; otherwise the string. `NextChainStep` / `NextChainStepForPress` return 0 for it. Suites: `StoryAirAttackTests`, `FighterAirAttackTests`. The new `gameplay_up` action (W / stick-up / dpad-up) feeds the vertical axis; Jump no longer contributes to it, so Warp-style up reads follow the Up input.
- **Basic-combo timing and chain rules live in `FTT.Combat.BasicComboRules`** (2026-08-09 cross-mode parity pass, revised same day for attack-on-the-move): per-hit startup/active/recovery frames (grounded totals 27/30/45; the aerial string's 25/28/40 was retired on 2026-10-12, and the air strike's frames are `AirAttackStartupFrames` / `AirAttackActiveFrames` / `AirAttackRecoveryFrames`), the 30/40/24-frame hitstun table (hits one and two hold a non-blocking victim through the buffered chain's connect-to-connect gaps — a grounded victim holding Block escapes hitstun into the stance, but as of the 2026-08-26 V7.3 pass only from hit two on: hit 1's hitstun is never block-cancelable), the 1x/1.2x/**4.5x** `KnockbackMultipliers` (the finisher launches the victim beyond melee range: `FighterKnockbackScalingTests` pins, for all nine kits, a separation of at least the larger of the gameplay-feel plan's 2.5-unit floor and that kit's own hit-1 reach + 0.5 unit — ledger `FIX-FINISHER-SEPARATION-BAR`; the tightest on 2026-10-11 is Cleopatra, 3.19 against 2.92), the 24-frame chain-hold/buffer window, the Story-enemy 24-frame stun floor for `combo_N` hits, the block-regen interval, and **since 2026-10-11 the grounded string hitbox geometry** in world units (`StringBoxHalfWidthUnits` / `StringBoxHalfHeightUnits` / `StringBoxOffsetXUnits` / `StringBoxOffsetYUnits`; the sim converts them with `FP64.FromDouble`, raw-identical to its old literals, and Story derives pixels at 60 px/unit through `PlayerController.ResolveGroundStringBoxPixels`; ledger `FIX-STRING-BOX-ONE-TABLE`). **Since D6b (2026-10-11, `USER-STORY-AERIAL-REACH`; `VERIFY-STORY-AERIAL-STRING-BOXES` closed)** a Story aerial string hit took its grounded twin's x-span, front-only (width % and the AttackRange minor included, facing read at active start; the `AerialHitboxMarker` latch is deleted), with only its vertical span Story-authored. **Since 2026-10-12 that rule lives on in the single air strike** (`PlayerController.ResolveAirAttackBoxPixels`: hit 1's x-span, the retired aerial hit 3's vertical span, `BasicComboRules.AirAttackBoxCentreYUnits` / `AirAttackBoxHeightUnits`, Y down from the feet); `ResolveAerialStringBoxPixels` and the per-hit `AerialStringBox*` arrays are deleted, and the sim's air strike reuses the grounded hit-1 box. The Up-Attack / Down-Air boxes are still separate (`VERIFY-DIRECTIONAL-HIT-MODEL`), and Story keeps hit 2 0.2 u *below* the hurtbox centre where the design and the sim lift it (`VERIFY-STORY-STRING-HIT2-LIFT`). Story's `PlayerController` timelines and the Fighter sim's phase machine (`FighterBasicAttackRules` in `scripts/FighterSim/FighterSimulationSystems.cs`) both consume it — never author a second copy of these numbers; `StringHitboxGeometryTests` fails on a Story far edge that is not the sim's × 60. Unified behavior in both modes: **swings keep full input steering** (grounded run ramp and aerial drift alike) with facing following held movement mid-swing (2026-08-10, superseding the whole-string facing commit; hitbox placement still reads facing at active-start); **held movement never cancels or resets the chain** (movement-cancel let a moving attacker restart hit one faster than the authored pace); jump/roll/block cancel the recovery and chain-hold window and reset the chain; a special cancels a swing as soon as the D1 Special gate (below) allows — at once after the swing lands, otherwise once its lockout has run out — and the ultimate once the lockout has run out; landing cancels an aerial swing (the air strike, the Down-Air) with no lag; being hit into hitstun cancels the string; whiffed swings chain only through the buffer/hold window; and knockback **replaces** velocity.
- Specials and ultimates use `AbilityData.BaseDamage`; multi-hit totals derive from per-hit damage and hit count. **Ultimate totals are `HitCount × BaseDamage + FinaleDamage`** (D15, Package 13 W6: `AbilityData.FinaleDamage` / `FinaleLaunches`, read by both modes through `FTT.Combat.UltimateActivationRules`): Einstein 78, Joan 76, Leonardo 74, Tesla 70 (+5 per detonated coil in its own arc radius, max 80), Shakespeare 76, Mozart 80, Cleopatra 78 (66 + a once-only Venom on the finale), Lincoln 70, Tubman 76. A Damage-slot status rides the finale only; a Control-slot hold rides the regular hits. W7a's burst-on-terrain projectile primitive (`AbilityData.ProjectileBurstRadius` / `ProjectileContactDamage` / `ProjectileBurstsOnTerrain`: `BaseDamage` is the burst, the contact stage is separate) and W7b's ground-wave primitive (`FighterGroundWave` on component 302 by type ID / `StoryGroundWave`; grounded-only waves skip airborne targets) are the two shared kit shapes.
- **The Ultimate activation strike (A02, Package 13 W6).** In the Fighter sim an accepted Ultimate spends the meter at once (even on a whiff), winds up 20 frames with hyper-armor (frozen if aerial), then strikes for 10 frames with an **unblockable** activation (Melee lunge, straight Projectile sweep or grounded-only GroundWave, per character: `AbilityData.ActivationShape` / `ActivationRange` / `ActivationHitboxSize` / `Activation*Frames`). It is avoided by roll, jump, air dodge, distance, Defy or an empty stock — **not** by block, Aegis or a barrier (an absorbed contact still counts). On contact the victim is held (`FighterUltimateActivationComponent`, **321**) while the character's existing Ultimate lands at full damage; on a whiff the caster sits in a 45-frame recovery that Echo Step, block, roll, jump and grab cannot undo. The world is not paused, only the two fighters (`VERIFY-ULT-CINEMATIC-PAUSE`). The CPU commits only inside 2 u against a grounded, non-invulnerable, non-rolling target. **Story keeps its screen-clearing Ultimates**; only the Level 13 Mirror clone plays the activation strike (`PlayerController.UsesFighterUltimateActivation`). Numbers are provisional (`VERIFY-ULT-ACTIVATION-TUNING`; decision 2.2 left the D15 totals for the pad session to judge). **Since 2026-10-16 the wind-up is telegraphed** (decision 2.1, `USER-ULT-TELEGRAPH`, `PROVISIONAL-ULT-TELEGRAPH-LOOK`; presentation only; `DEFER-ULT-TELEGRAPH` closed): for exactly the wind-up of a live, free caster the sprite holds a steady Unblockable-red tint (the glow arbiter's dedicated `SetUltimateTelegraph` flag, which a hit flash rides on top of and the hero aura never warms) and shows the Unblockable X glyph at (0, −120) px, radius 14, through `FTT.Combat.UltimateActivationTelegraph` — in Fighter derived from component 321 every sync by `FighterSimulationDriver.UltimateTelegraph.cs` (it drops on the knockout tick, CM-FIXA-PRES), on the Mirror clone at its activation phase edges by `PlayerController.UltimateTelegraph.cs` — plus one Critical cue on the wind-up's first frame, `CoreMechanicCueIDs.UltimateActivationWindup` (`ultimate_activation_windup`, a silent placeholder stream). The ruling said "red flash"; it is built steady, so Reduced Temporal Effects changes nothing (an open question). A clone that leaves `UsingUltimate` by any edge (a stun, a death, a grab) now resets its activation phase (`FIX-MIRROR-ULT-PHASE-EXIT`). On the thaw tick of a hitstop that froze an A02 caster in its wind-up or whiff recovery no grab, movement ability or other action starts, and its DI read survives (CM-FIXA-SIM ruling (c); `VERIFY-SIM-A02-THAW-TICK`, closed). A fallen caster no longer wears the gold hyper-armour outline (`VERIFY-FIGHTER-KO-HYPERARMOR-GLOW-TICK`, closed). **Story Ultimates are armoured for their own Startup since 2026-10-16** (decision 2.4 with Q2 "startup only"; `USER-STORY-ULTIMATE-ARMOR`, `USER-STORY-ULTIMATE-ARMOR-STARTUP-ONLY`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-ULTIMATE-ARMOR`): a Story rule, `StoryCombatRules.UltimateStartupArmorPreventsInterruption` (Startup, any incoming class but Ultimate), keyed to the cast's own clock so hitstop and Time Freeze pause it, OR'd into `PlayerController.HasActiveHyperArmor` (`PlayerController.UltimateArmor.cs`; the gold shell shows through the startup); the nine `ultimate.tres` keep `GrantsHyperArmor = false`, so Fighter is unchanged. A hit's StaticCharge no longer stuns through any armour window (`ArmorAbsorbsStatusStun`; the charge still enters the control slot), and Einstein's Rift Collapse no longer launches an armoured body (its pull still moves one). The Level 13 clone's post-contact Story Ultimate is armoured the same way (`VERIFY-STORY-MIRROR-HOLD`).
- Knockback target: `baseKnockback / (1 + target.Weight)`. **In Fighter Mode only** it is then scaled by the victim's missing HP after the hit — `×(1 + missingHPFraction)`, linear 1× at full HP to 2× at zero (sim chokepoint `FighterDamageRules.ApplyFighterHit` / `ScaleByMissingHP`; `BasicComboRules.LowHealthKnockbackScale` is its float statement, which nothing outside the sim and the rulebook reads). **Story knockback is static since 2026-10-12** (user request 5, ledger `USER-STORY-STATIC-KNOCKBACK`, `PROVISIONAL-STATIC-KNOCKBACK-RULES`): `DamageCalculator`'s victim-HP overload is deleted, and every Story victim — the hero, the Mirror clone, a thrown body, mobs, elites, bowled bodies — takes `DamageCalculator.CalculateKnockback(base, weight, attackerFacingRight)` at any HP and on any difficulty, so whether a hit wall-bounces a mob depends on the hero and the mob, never on its HP. `BossController.ApplyKnockback` was already static but still adds to velocity with a hard-coded weight of 2 (the remaining recorded inconsistency). No Fighter hash moved. There is no armor/defense stat that reduces HP damage.
- **The ability lockout, a special global cooldown (user direction 2026-10-08, both modes; ledger `USER-ABILITY-LOCKOUT` and `PROVISIONAL-ABILITY-LOCKOUT-RULES`), and the hit-confirm cancel (user rulings 2026-10-11, D1 / D1a; `USER-HIT-CONFIRM-CANCEL`, `PROVISIONAL-HIT-CONFIRM-RULES`).** Starting any basic swing (each chain hit, buffered and held continuations included, the up-attack, the down-air), casting Special 1 or Special 2, or having the Ultimate accepted arms one per-fighter lockout **A** of `BasicComboRules.AbilityLockoutFrames` (30). **The Ultimate is refused while A runs**, after every swing, landed or not, in both modes — there is no hit-confirm for the Ultimate (in Fighter it would make the activation strike undodgeable). **Specials read `BasicComboRules.SpecialGateFrames(A, S, C) = C ? S : A`:** **S** is a second, Special-sourced lockout armed to 30 with A by every Special 1/2 and Ultimate acceptance and ticking with it; **C** is a swing-landed latch cleared by every swing start and set by that swing's first *landed* contact. So a swing that lands releases Special 1/2 at once (the first legal confirm after the template opener is T+10 in the sim and T+12 in Story, against T+34 before), a whiff still locks for 30, and Special → Special keeps the full 30 whatever lands in between. **Landed (D1a)** means damage was applied, or a shield or barrier layer absorbed it — never a block-stance block, an invulnerability / arena-ward / Time Freeze / world-hold refusal, a whiff, or a construct, prop or strike surface, and never a payload with zero authored damage (the orchestrator's ruling). In Story the receiver reports it (`Hurtbox.ReportLanded` / `DidLand`, read back into `HitPayload.ContactLanded` by `Hitbox.OnAreaEntered`) from `EnemyController`, `BossController` (after the ward and every invulnerability window), `PlayerController.ResolveIncomingHit` (a full absorption counts only on a body that is not post-rewind, tech or knockdown invulnerable, `IsConfirmInvulnerable`) and `TrainingDummy`; in the sim `FighterDamageRules.ResolveFighterHit` returns `Refused` / `Blocked` / `Absorbed` / `Landed`, and `ApplyFighterHit` is its hash-neutral `== Landed` wrapper. A refused press spends no cooldown or meter and arms nothing. Basics are never refused, so the string chains as before; the movement ability, roll, air dodge, grab and Echo Step neither arm nor read either lockout. Both are armed at acceptance and tick with the ability cooldowns, so they pause in the fighter's own hitstop (and under Time Freeze in Story). **Per-hit Momentum (D2, `USER-PER-HIT-MOMENTUM`) replaced the V7.1 finisher-only refund:** every landed swing refunds **both** running Special cooldowns — 20 frames for string hits 1–2, the Up-Attack and the Down-Air, 60 for the finisher — capped at 120 frames refunded per slot per cooldown cycle, paid once per swing however many bodies it cleaves, bosses included (`BasicComboRules.MomentumGrant`; Story sets a timer the grant fully covers to exactly 0). **The freeze-only Special buffer** (user ruling, `USER-FREEZE-SPECIAL-BUFFER`; scope `PROVISIONAL-FREEZE-SPECIAL-BUFFER`): a Special 1/2 press inside the attacker freeze of the fighter's own landed swing is held one deep and re-presented on the thaw tick (Story's first unfrozen tick, the sim's last freeze tick, which its systems read), where the ordinary gates judge it; nothing else is buffered and the Ultimate never is. **The "not yet" flash (D10, `USER-NOT-YET-FLASH`):** a Special 1, Special 2 or Ultimate press the lockout alone refused flashes that slot's HUD icon in both modes — Story raises `EventBus.OnAbilityLockoutRefused` at the refusal site, `FighterSimulationDriver` classifies each local tick with the pure, engine-free `FighterLockoutRefusalRules.Evaluate` (presentation only, never an ISystem, none in LAN), and both HUDs play `FTT.UI.NotYetFlash` (a 0.25 s `UIPalette.BossRed` `SelfModulate` tint plus a contracting 2 px frame, the non-colour channel). There is still no lockout timer on screen. **Same-tick order, both modes:** a basic swing, then an Ultimate the meter pays for, then Special 1, then Special 2. The basic swing includes a chain continuation that starts that tick (`PlayerController.ChainSwingStartsThisTick` mirrors the sim, which starts swings in `FighterMovementSystem` before any ability is polled). The paid Ultimate also takes the tick from the movement ability, because the default gamepad Ultimate is the Movement + Special 2 chord (`FighterCombatSystem.UltimatePressAccepted`); since D1 a paid Ultimate the lockout alone holds back still claims the tick from Special 1/2, never from the movement ability (Story `_ultimateHeldBackThisTick`, sim `FighterCombatSystem.UltimatePressHeldBackByLockout`), so the LB+RB chord inside a hit-confirm cannot cast its Special 2. An accepted special also takes the tick from the movement ability, as Story polls it first. A fighter that an activation strike captures this tick takes no further action that tick. **Since 2026-10-16 the sim also refuses the Ultimate** in the block stance (shieldstun included), the landing tech's lock, the fighter's own Special cast, a knockdown and a busy A02 caster, through the one predicate `FighterCombatSystem.UltimatePressPayable` that every caller and the refusal classifier read, so a refused stance press claims nothing and never flashes (decision 2.3, `USER-NO-ULT-FROM-BLOCK`; Story already dropped it; `VERIFY-SAME-TICK-VERB-PARITY` (1) closed, and its new item (9) is the tick Block is released, where the sim can still accept one). Story: `PlayerController.AbilityLockoutFramesRemaining`, `SpecialGateFramesRemaining` and `SwingLanded`; never persisted, never scaled by Story cooldown perks, and cleared (S, C and the buffer with A) by a death rewind and a checkpoint restore. Sim: `FighterRuntimeComponent.AbilityLockoutFrames`, component 305's first int (the unread `BasicCooldownFrames` reused 2026-10-08), and the D1 word packed into 305's unread `AegisHits` int (S in bits 0–15, C in 16, the freeze buffer in 17–19), so 305 is still exactly 128 B and no ID was allocated; the rules live in `FighterAbilityLockoutRules` (`CanReadAbilityPress` is the ability system's shared read gate). Sudden Death zeroes them; a stock loss lets A and S expire, keeps C and drops a held buffer press. The CPU reads `CpuDecisionObservation.AbilityLockoutFrames` for its Ultimate and, since D1, `SpecialLockoutFrames` / `SwingLanded` for its Special rungs and its mobility Special (`SpecialLockoutClearAtDelivery`; the Mirror fills all three from Story), as they will stand at delivery, meaning at most the band's minimum reaction delay. Its Echo Step meter valuation ignores the lockout, and while a viable Ultimate waits on the lockout the CPU stops feeding its string — so a full-meter Hard CPU never hit-confirms against a grounded target in hitstun (`PROVISIONAL-HIT-CONFIRM-RULES` (7)). **Since 2026-10-16 the CPU also reads its own Special cast** (decision 9.3, "only adapt it to the new Special timing"; `USER-CPU-SPECIAL-TIMING-ONLY`, `PROVISIONAL-CPU-SPECIAL-TIMING`): `CpuDecisionObservation.SelfSpecialCastPhase` (the mode-neutral `CpuCastPhase` scale) and `SelfSpecialCastPhaseFrames`, with `SelfTechLockoutFrames`, `SelfKnockedDown` and `SelfUltimateCasterBusy`; both lockout predicates also require `CastIdleAtDelivery` (no cast, or a recovery with no more frames left than the band's minimum reaction delay), a Special 1, Special 2 or Ultimate edge is dropped at delivery while the cast holds the tick, and the grab and Echo Step mirrors refuse during the cast (the grab mirror also in the tech lock, a knockdown and its own Ultimate); the Mirror projects the clone's executing Special (`PlayerController.ExecutingSpecialCast`). Kit awareness stays out (`VERIFY-CPU-SPLITTING-STRIKE-REACH`). Every Fighter hash moved (per-hit Momentum, CPU Specials mid-string). In Fighter PvP hit 1 → any reaching Special is now a guaranteed confirm, accepted under D1 and owed a judgement against F09; since Fighter Specials gained their startup (2026-10-16) hit 1's 30-frame hitstun still covers every Special startup (at most 16), so it stays a true confirm, and a Special press during the fighter's own cast is discarded, never refused. Older Story/sim same-tick differences are listed under `VERIFY-SAME-TICK-VERB-PARITY` (its item (7) records the two D1 made player-facing). Suites: `AbilityLockoutRulesTests`, `StoryAbilityLockoutTests`, `FighterAbilityLockoutTests`, `FighterHitConfirmTests`, `StoryHitConfirmTests`, `FighterLockoutRefusalRulesTests`, `StoryLockoutRefusalEventTests`.
- **Fighter Specials run their authored startup, active and recovery (decision 1.2, "Full timing now", 2026-10-16; ledger `USER-SIM-SPECIAL-TIMING`, `PROVISIONAL-SIM-SPECIAL-PHASE-RULES`; `DEFER-SIM-SPECIAL-PHASES` and `SIM-SPECIAL-POSE-THIRDS` closed).** Until then the sim resolved every Special on its press tick. Now `FighterAbilityEntitySystem` accepts a press — spending the cooldown, resetting the Momentum counter, arming the lockout and cancelling the string at once, Special 1 before Special 2 — and starts a cast on component 323 (`FighterSpecialCastRules.Begin`; `FighterMovementSystem.AdvanceSpecialCast` clocks it after the hitstop gate). Press T is the first startup tick; the slot's effect fires on T+S in the fire pass (`FighterAbilityEntitySystem.FireActiveSpecials`: projectile, construct, zone, the Rift placement, the Vortex anchor, the Tempest lift); a melee Special tests contact on every active tick until it resolves one (`FighterCombatSystem.BuildIntent`); and the caster is locked through T+S+A+R−1 — its input discarded on every tick, its own hitstop's freeze and thaw ticks included, `CanStartGrab`, `CanReadAbilityPress` and the Ultimate predicate refusing, no ledge capture — and free on T+S+A+R. Story fires the same effect on T+S−1, one tick earlier (`VERIFY-SAME-TICK-VERB-PARITY` (8)). Hitstun, a daze, a grab (either side), an A02 capture, a knockdown, a KO or the respawn platform clears the cast (`FighterSpecialCastRules.IsInterrupted`); a hit landing on T+S trades, because the fire pass ran first. The frames are loadout data (`FighterAbilityHitData.StartupFrames` / `RecoveryFrames`, projected by `FighterLoadoutFactory`), never snapshot state, and a contract with no authored active frames (a hand-built `FighterAbilityLoadout.Default` slot) runs 0 / 1 / 0, the old press-tick timing. **Every accepted press spends** (1.1(2), `FIX-SPLITTING-STRIKE-FREE-WHIFF`): an out-of-reach Splitting Strike plays and whiffs, and it wins a same-tick movement press. **Shield-Breakers keep the full shatter and gain their startup** (1.3, `USER-SHIELD-BREAKER-STARTUP`): measured, after a blocked Divine Piercing (13 / 12 / 20), Emancipator (16 / 6 / 24) or Splitting Strike (14 / 6 / 22) the attacker's first Ultimate press comes 39 / 45 / 32 frames before the blocker can act (shatter + 76), longer than the 20-frame wind-up, so a blocked Shield-Breaker is still an Ultimate punish. **Cast motion (Q1, "Brake to a stop, both modes"; `USER-SPECIAL-CAST-BRAKE`):** a grounded cast brakes to 0 over the 4-frame run-stop ramp (`UniversalMovementRules.RunDecelerationFrames`); an aerial cast keeps its air drift, with no stick and no fast-fall; a kit that writes its own velocity (Divine Piercing's lunge on exactly its 12 active ticks, the Tempest lift, a planted Foresight) overrides the brake; and Story's `ProcessUsingSpecial` brakes the same way (`PlayerController.ApplySpecialCastBrake`), so a running Story caster no longer slides at its entry speed. A cast that started airborne falls at `UniversalMovementRules.AerialSpecialGravityMultiplier` 0.5 through its startup and active frames in both modes (Story's `ProcessUsingSpecial` forwards to it; the sim's `FighterAirPhysics.AerialSpecialGravityScale`, Foresight's startup and window included). The driver poses `special_1` / `special_2` from 323's phases for the whole cast; the thirds of the 18-frame cosmetic hold stay only for the fallback and the press-tick Ultimate edge. Suites: `FighterSpecialCastRulesTests`, `FighterSpecialTimingTests`, `ShieldBreakerTimingTests`, `StorySpecialTimingProbeTests`, `StorySpecialCastMotionTests`.
- A special may cancel a basic attack when allowed by the FSM/cooldown rules and the ability lockout. Block may cancel only during recovery frames — a basic's recovery, and since Package 12 W3 a **Special's** recovery too (grounded, never startup or active; the armed cooldown stands). The sim had no special recovery lock until 2026-10-16, so its stance simply rose on the next tick; now its recovery Block cancel is grounded, opens on recovery frame 0 and keeps only the Block press on its tick, so every other verb needs a fresh press (CM-FIXA-SIM ruling (d)), and Foresight's whiff recovery and sidestep gain it. **Since 2026-10-11 a Special that landed a direct hit cancels its own recovery (D3, Story only until 2026-10-16, both modes since; `USER-SPECIAL-RECOVERY-CANCEL`, `PROVISIONAL-SPECIAL-RECOVERY-CANCEL-RULES`):** from recovery frame 0 into a roll (the air dodge when airborne) or a jump, from frame 6 into a basic attack — hit 1, the Up-Attack or the Down-Air by the direction held at the press, so a held direction never eats the press (the addendum) — or the movement ability. "Landed" is the D1a predicate on a `DirectHit` payload of the same execution (`BaseSpecial.LandedDirectHitThisExecution`, set by `ConfirmAbilityHit`'s first statement; ticks, constructs, zones and a late shot from an earlier cast never arm it), and recovery frames count the cast clock, paused in hitstop (`BaseSpecial.RecoveryFramesElapsed`). A one-slot, 6-tick cancel buffer is fed on every `UsingSpecial` tick (hitstop ticks never age it); a tick whose Ultimate bit is pressed never takes or keeps the movement cancel, and the Echo Step chord's Roll never rolls (A06: no Echo Step out of a Special). The verb starts, then the cast is interrupted; what the cast put into the world, its cooldown and its lockout stand. A whiff keeps its whole recovery, and neither a Special nor the Ultimate is polled there, so Special → Special stays lockout-gated. Foresight's earliest cancel is frame 1, and a cancel (or the old Block cancel) now ends its post-counter sidestep. Cleopatra's two Specials and the Tempest never qualify. The Level 13 Mirror clone inherits it. **Since 2026-10-16 D3 is one rule in both modes** (decision 11.1, `USER-FIGHTER-SPECIAL-RECOVERY-CANCEL`, `PROVISIONAL-FIGHTER-SPECIAL-RECOVERY-CANCEL`): the table lives once in `BasicComboRules`' D3 section (`SpecialRecoveryEvadeCancelFrame` 0, `SpecialRecoveryActionCancelFrame` 6, `SpecialCancelBufferFrames` 6 and their predicates), which `StoryCombatRules` forwards. The sim keeps its state on component 323 — `SpecialFlags` 8 `FlagLandedDirectHit`, armed by a `Landed` or `Absorbed` contact with damage from the casting slot's own `DirectHit` delivery (Golden Ratio, the Lorentz Pulse and the Requiem burst arm it; ticks, constructs and Cleopatra's Specials never do), and `SpecialCancelBuffer`, with Story's verb order and exclusions — and fires it in the cast region after the Block cancel, through each verb's ordinary path (`FighterSpecialCancelRules`, `FighterMovementSystem.SpecialCancel.cs`). Neither recovery cancel ends Foresight while its counter answer is still owed, in either mode (CM-FIXB-SIM B12; the cancel waits one tick). Suites: `StorySpecialRecoveryCancelTests`, one case in `StoryCombatRulesTests`, `FighterSpecialRecoveryCancelTests`, `SpecialRecoveryCancelTableTests`.
- Blocking stops movement and is front-facing. Three basic hits exhaust the three shield charges; **a blocked Special costs two charges** (`min(2, charges)`, with the ordinary shieldstun when it does not shatter — 8 frames until 2026-10-16, scaled by damage since, below — A01, Package 13 W1, both modes, via `BlockRules.ChargeCost` and `BasicComboRules.SpecialBlockChargeCost`, never via `BlockChargeCost = 2`, which would trip Shield of Orléans' refund); a **Shield-Breaker** Special (`BlockClass.ShieldBreaker = 4`, appended: Divine Piercing, The Emancipator, Splitting Strike) consumes every charge; the Ultimate's activation strike is unblockable. Story construct hits (serpent nest, the Cataclysm coil burst) are Basic-class. Guard break causes a one-second daze. Both modes enforce the grounded block stance (2026-08-09): no absorb while airborne, mid-swing, or mid-roll; attack and ability inputs are ignored while the stance is up; and one charge regenerates per `BasicComboRules.BlockChargeRegenFrames` (180) while not blocking, in the sim as well as Story. **The V7.3 block-model closure landed 2026-08-26** (closing audit H-5; both modes): a non-shatter blocked hit locks the blocker into the stance for its shieldstun (`ShieldstunFrames` 8 until 2026-10-16, `ShieldstunFramesFor(damage)` since — no drop, grab, roll, jump, or drop-through, and grabs *whiff* against a shieldstunned target); a shatter freezes both parties for `ShatterFreezeFrames` (16, replacing the blocked 2f hitstop) and arms `BlockShatterLockoutFrames` (300) during which the stance cannot rise and regen is held (charge #1 at shatter+480f; stock loss clears it — **a charge restore never ends the lockout**, V7.6 F17 deletes that rule, and `RestoreAllCharges()` no longer zeroes the lockout timer); an empty shield never raises the stance; the 2026-08-10 hitstun block-cancel is gated to hits two and three of the string (hit 1 arms `HitstunBlockCancelBlocked`); a grabbing or held fighter has no functioning shield (attack beats grab); and landing tech reads the raw Block input, unaffected by charges or the lockout. The 2.0 s design regen figure stays retired (3.0 s, settled 2026-08-22). **Guard-break push (Package 12 W3):** a shatter **assigns** (never adds) an unscaled push away from the attacker's side — `BasicComboRules.GuardBreakPushX` 2.0 / `GuardBreakPushYDown` −1.0, Story `BlockSystem.GuardBreakPushVelocity` ±120 / −60 px/s — in both modes. **Since 2026-10-16, both modes** (decisions 4.1 / 11.2 and 3.4 with Q3 "Still shatters"; ledger `USER-SCALED-SHIELDSTUN`, `PROVISIONAL-SHIELDSTUN-CURVE`, `USER-PERFECT-BLOCK`, `USER-SHIELD-BREAKER-BEATS-PERFECT-BLOCK`, `PROVISIONAL-PERFECT-BLOCK-RULES`): **shieldstun scales with the blocked hit's damage**, `BasicComboRules.ShieldstunFramesFor(damage)` = clamp(`ShieldstunBaseFrames` 6 + damage / `ShieldstunDamageDivisor` 4, 6, `ShieldstunMaxFrames` = `ShatterFreezeFrames` 16) with integer division (a basic of 4–7 damage → 7, Lincoln's 13-damage finisher → 9, Righteous Smite's 28 → 13, the Emancipator's 40 → 16, a zero-damage cast → 6), on the damage left after any barrier (in Story after partial absorption, with the difficulty multiplier the mob's damage already carries); the flat `ShieldstunFrames` and `BlockSystem.ShieldStunSeconds` are deleted, and Story's shieldstun runs on the hero's own clock through a Time Freeze while regen and the shatter lockout still hold (CM-FIXB-STORY B4). **A perfect block** — the stance raised within `PerfectBlockWindowFrames` 5 of contact (contact ages 0–4 from the first live stance tick), armed only by a down → up stance edge with Block pressed that tick and the stance lowered either never this stock or for at least `PerfectBlockRearmFrames` 20 frames (`BasicComboRules.PerfectBlockArms`) — spends no charge, sets no shieldstun and re-arms no regen delay. The 2-frame blocked hitstop stays and the outcome is still `Blocked` (no D1a confirm, no Momentum; in Story it opens the D8 Punish window and grants Henry's Bastion, while Shield of Orléans pays nothing); a Shield-Breaker still shatters (Q3) and a Guard-Crush (an authored cost of at least `GuardCrushBlockChargeCost` 2, now the one statement of that value) keeps its effect (`PerfectBlockApplies`); there is no reflect and no attacker stun, so Foresight stays the true counter. A held raise, the hit-2 escape's stance and a stance raised during the fighter's own hitstop never arm; hitstop pauses the window and the lowered age, and a Story Time Freeze does not (CM-FIXB-STORY B6, which reversed the plan's choice). The sim's clock is component 323's `BlockTiming` (`FighterBlockTimingRules` in `FighterMovementSystem.Block.cs`; a tick that starts in hitstun or daze counts as lowered, CM-FIXB-SIM B5), threaded to every blockable hit site, the ledge attack's included (`fa18442`); Story's is `PlayerController.PerfectBlock.cs`. Both modes flash `BlockSystem.PerfectBlockFlashColor` (0.85, 1, 1) for 0.12 s on the hit-flash channel, with the ordinary block's shake and the Guard Impact haptic. The Calibration Drill's block string counts a perfect block as a block, and `systems_card_block_body` states both rules. Suites: `ShieldstunCurveTests`, `FighterPerfectBlockTests`, `StoryPerfectBlockTests`.
- **The V7.6 defensive contract D01–D04 landed 2026-09-13 (Package 11 A1b, both modes).** Per distinct eligible hit contact the resolution order is **hit eligibility / invulnerability → the D04 Defy window → projectile immunity → Temporal Aegis → HP barrier → ordinary block → HP**, stopping at the first layer that fully prevents the hit. **Full absorption spends no block charge and applies no stun, knockback, status or combo mark** — including at exact depletion; partial absorption passes only the remainder, at the attack's original `AttackClass` / `BlockChargeCost`. Story routes the hurtbox path, DoT ticks and hazard ticks through the one shared `PlayerController.ResolveProtectionLayers`; the sim does the same above `ApplyFighterHit`'s block branch. **D04:** a Defy proc releases the survivor from the triggering hit's hitstun, launch, tumble, stashed DI and capture, zeroes that hit's knockback/hitstun/status, and grants **60 active ticks** of protection counted from the first resumed normal-control tick (ticks 1–60 protected, tick 61 not), paused under hitstop / Time Freeze / suspended presentations. **Since 2026-10-16 it ends early on the survivor's first attack, Special, Ultimate or grab** in both modes (decision 4.3, "Ends on your first attack or Special, both modes"; `USER-DEFY-ENDS-ON-OFFENCE`: the sim's `FighterDefenseRules.EndOnOffence` from the action chokepoint, Story's `ClearDefyProtection` at every swing start, Special or Ultimate acceptance and successful grab), and a defied hit writes no velocity in either mode (CM-FIXB-SIM B2); the sim still cancels the survivor's own roll or air dodge on a defied blow (`VERIFY-DEFY-CANCELS-OWN-ROLL`, open). A Sudden Death Venom tick now respects `DefyBarred` (1.1(4), `FIX-SIM-VENOM-DEFY-BARRED`: one `FighterDefenseRules.CanDefy` for both damage paths). The perfect block (the block bullet above) is a variant of the ordinary block layer, so the D01/D02b order is unchanged. It is not immunity to a non-hit death — `KillPlayerNonHit` is untouched. **D02a** grants are identity-keyed: a repeat grant of the same effect refills to cap and restarts the timer, never adds, and a repeated `grantEventId` is refused outright; granted shields live `GrantedShieldLifetimeFrames` (480). **D02d** Wardenclyffe recharges at 2.5 % of MaxHP per live second (empty→full is exactly 6 s at any MaxHP), arms a 3-second damage delay on capacity *or* HP loss, and absorbs **only inside an owned coil radius**. **D02e** Story gained Temporal Aegis (`OrbEffect.TemporalAegis`) as an active flag, never a charge count. **D03d** a throw bypasses finite shields (`HitPayload.BypassesFiniteShields`). **F15** Divine Piercing and The Emancipator shatter the whole shield in both modes — since Package 13 through the explicit `ShieldBreaker` block class rather than as ordinary Specials (A01 made an ordinary Special cost two charges).
- **Rally and meter origin (D03f–D03h).** Rally reclaim heals `min(pool, credited × 2.0, missingHP)` and debits only the HP actually healed, so a full-health attacker no longer burns its pool for nothing. **Zone ticks never reclaim** (the sim zone pulse and all three expiry launches pass `collectsEcho: false`; Story reads it off the authored `HitDelivery.Tick` through `HitClassification.CollectsEcho` since Package 12 W3/W4). **Ultimate-origin damage earns the caster zero damage-dealt meter** — `AddInfluenceFromDamageDealt(dealt, collectsEcho, ultimateOrigin)`, routed from every kit script through `BaseSpecial.CreditDealt` (Package 12 W4), `Hitbox` and `PlaceholderZone` (via the **authored** `HitPayload.Origin` / `AbilityData.Origin` since Package 12 W3 — no longer the attack class or the slot), the sim zone pulse, `ApplyIntent`'s ultimate branch and the Cataclysm coil chain.
- **Echo Step is exact (V7.6, Package 11 A1c).** The retired ring held 5 samples written every 6 frames and read the oldest, so the destination was 24–30 frames back depending on phase. It is now **31 consecutive per-tick position samples and the exact `t − 30` one** — with 31 slots the write head *is* the `t − 30` slot, so there is no search, rounding or interpolation. Sampling is hoisted above the hitstop short-circuit, so an advancing hitstop tick records the same position. Generations open at spawn, at a stock loss and at Sudden Death setup, and spawn-filled storage is never counted as history: a fighter must genuinely live 30 further ticks. **Exact-or-no-teleport:** the destination is validated *before* anything is spent (an invalid or unavailable one refuses at no meter and no cooldown cost and does not start the wind-up) and rechecked at wind-up completion (a destination that became blocked **cancels with the committed cost retained** — no refund, no nearby substitute, no added immunity). Story mirrors all of it on `PlayerController`.
- **Same-frame chord priority** is the single shared table `BasicComboRules.SelectRecoveryVerb`: **the chord beats the single input, and the priced verb beats the free one.** A chord whose priced verb is unavailable falls back to the block-cancel rather than eating the input. Before this the ordering was incidental — the sim happened to call `TryStartEchoStep` eight lines before the roll cancel and Story called all three verbs from three separate sites.
- **Fighter modes are Stock or Time only (F21).** `MatchMode.Hybrid = 2` is `[Obsolete]`, reserved and **removed from both selectors**; a legacy saved `Mode == 2` normalizes to timed Stock with a valid positive timer. **Time is decided by fewest stocks lost**, with unlimited respawns and **no HP tiebreak** — `FighterMatchComponent` now counts victim-side `PlayerOneStocksLost` / `PlayerTwoStocksLost` fed from the single `ApplyStockLoss` chokepoint (it used to count the KOs a player *scored* and pick the larger, the opposite bookkeeping). Equal totals, 0-0 included, enter Sudden Death.
- **Sudden Death (F22)** is a shared round reset. Retired: the **1-HP clamp**, the **forced-hazards-on** override, and the **pre-marked Defy flag**. Living fighters keep their exact current positive HP (unequal included); a fighter dead or respawning at the end of regulation gets its normal respawn HP once, recorded on component 319 so a duplicate transition cannot grant it twice. Hazards respect the house toggle — **Off stays Off**; On runs twice-normal cadence with a full first warning. Meter 0, every cooldown ready, block refilled, both status slots / Rally pool / marks cleared, regulation projectiles/zones/constructs/hazards swept, orbs disabled, invulnerability cleared. Defy is disabled by **derivation** from `SuddenDeathActive`, so the spent flag is preserved and entry consumes no use. Regulation stocks-lost totals are frozen on component 319 and reach `MatchResults`.
- **Resonance Barrier (A11, Package 13 W1, Story).** One shared `StoryShieldEffect.ResonanceBarrier` (appended = 5) — 10 % of max HP, 8 s, a repeat grant refills rather than stacks, one instance per recipient, the source recorded — granted by Henry's Bastion, Royal Aegis and Tubman's **North Star Ward** (`NorthStarWard = 6`; `LeafBarrier = 3` is `[Obsolete]`, reserved). `PlayerController.GrantResonanceBarrier(source, grantEventId)`; `BlockSystem`'s duplicate `0.10f` is gone. Wardenclyffe, Aegis and projectile immunity are unchanged.
- **Pushbox and grab geometry (A13, Package 13 W1, both modes).** Pushboxes are **0.6 units** (Story player 36 px at 60 px/unit; sim minimum separation 0.6). The grab is a box from the pivot to 0.8 forward tested against the target's **hurtbox** (template 0.8, Lincoln 0.9, Cleopatra 0.7 wide), so it connects at pushbox contact and out to 1.2 between pivots. Enemy/boss scene pushboxes were not narrowed (`VERIFY-ENEMY-PUSHBOX-WIDTH`) and ordinary sim hits still test the 1.0-wide hurtbox (`VERIFY-SIM-HURTBOX-WIDTH`). **Since 2026-10-16 each mode states its hurtbox once, with no number changed** (decision 8.4, `USER-HURTBOX-ONE-VALUE`; the target size still waits for data): the sim's 1.0 × 2.0 u is `FighterHurtbox.HalfExtents` (with the separately named alias `FighterBodyOverlap.HalfExtents` for orb pickup and hazard overlap; the ten copies are gone, `FighterHurtboxSourceScanTests`), and Story's string-box anchor is derived from `StoryCombatRules.HeroHurtboxHeightPixels` while the factory's 40 × 64 px body box is named apart (`CharacterFactory.HeroBodyBoxWidthPixels` / `HeroBodyBoxHeightPixels`; `VERIFY-STORY-HURTBOX-UNIVERSAL`).
- **Stock loss despawns (A04, Package 13 W1, Fighter only).** On the KO tick every persistent object and zone the fallen fighter owns is destroyed (`FighterStockLossRules`); projectiles already in flight finish. Story keeps persistence. **Signed knockback (D10):** the sim projects signed `KnockbackX/Y` for Special intents, projectiles, zone pulses and the Down-Air slam instead of flattening to `max(|x|,|y|)` — Ultimates still use the scalar (`DEFER-ULT-KNOCKBACK-SCALAR`). **Root** (A05/D11) also refuses the air dodge and the sim movement ability. **A06:** Story Special recovery no longer arms Echo Step; Echo Step keys to the basic swing in both modes.
- The Influence/Ultimate meter ranges from 0 to 100. Damage dealt adds 1 point per HP; damage taken adds 0.25 per HP. Using the ultimate resets it. Fighter stock loss retains 75% of the current meter. **These four numbers live once, in `FTT.Combat.InfluenceMeterRules`** (2026-10-11; Godot-free integer rationals: `Cap` 100, dealt 1/1, taken 1/4, stock-loss retention 3/4). Story reads its exact float views and its two gain functions (`GainFromDamageDealt`, `GainFromDamageTaken(damage, gainFactor)`); `UltimateMeter`'s `MaxValue` / `PointsPerDamage*` / `StockLossRetention` are forwarders. The sim reads it only through `FTT.FighterSim.FighterMeterRules` (`CapPoints`, `Cap`, `FromDamageDealt`, `FromDamageTaken`, `RetainOnStockLoss`), which repeats the retired fixed-point operations exactly, so every Fighter result is bit-identical (90 seeded CPU matches compared hash for hash). `InfluenceMeterSourceScanTests` fails on a stray meter cap or rate literal in the former copy sites or anywhere under `scripts/FighterSim/`, and on the sim naming the Story gain factor. **Mozart's Minor Resonance (`UltimateBuildRate`, +10 %) is wired** (ledger `FIX-MOZART-BUILD-RATE-UNREAD`): `PlayerController.StoryMeterGainFactor` is the lane's only reader, applied by `UltimateMeter.AddFromDamageDealt` / `AddFromDamageTaken` (the Rally echo as it drains included) and by `RallyUncreditedMeter`, never to flat grants, direct sets, spends, drains, stock-loss retention or Ultimate-origin damage, and never in the sim (`PROVISIONAL-STORY-METER-GAIN-SCOPE`). **Since 2026-10-11 the Story campaign hero earns every meter gain ×0.35 (D5, user direction `USER-STORY-METER-SCALE`; details `PROVISIONAL-STORY-METER-SCALE-RULES`):** damage dealt, damage taken and the drained Rally echo, times Mozart's build-rate lane, through `PlayerController.StoryMeterGainFactor` = `StoryCombatRules.MeterGainFactor(AppliesStoryMeterGainScale, lane)` with `StoryCombatRules.MeterGainScale` = 0.35. `PlayerController.AppliesStoryMeterGainScale` (never persisted) is set only by `CharacterFactory`'s campaign-hero branch (`applyStoryProgression && applyLegacyUnlockLocks`); every other build — the Level 13 Mirror clone (written false; a Hard clone keeps the Mozart lane it mirrors, 1.1), the drills, the Holodeck, the Test Arena and Fighter — gets no Story scale. Flat grants, direct sets and spends are unchanged, and the Story-only constant never enters `InfluenceMeterRules` or `scripts/FighterSim/` (`InfluenceMeterSourceScanTests`). Defy History and Echo Step come around less often, an accepted side effect: the bots' clean bar fill went from about 14 s to about 38 s and their Ultimates per Normal run from 7.1 to 2.8. A test that fills a campaign hero's meter through a gain now gets 35, not 100: fill with `SetUltimateMeter(InfluenceMeterRules.MaxValue)`. Suites: `StoryMeterGainScaleTests`, one case each in `InfluenceMeterSourceScanTests` and `MirrorParadoxTests`. **The campaign hero's Echo Step costs 30 × 0.35 = 10.5 since 2026-10-16** (decision 7.3, "Story cost ×0.35 (about 10–11 meter)"; `USER-STORY-ECHO-STEP-COST`): `StoryCombatRules.StoryEchoStepMeterCost` = `BasicComboRules.EchoStepMeterCost × MeterGainScale`, a derived `const float`, read through `PlayerController.EchoStepMeterCost` (`StoryCombatRules.EchoStepMeterCost(AppliesStoryMeterGainScale)`); every other build — the Mirror clone, the drills, the Holodeck, Fighter and the bots' `--story-meter-scale=off` A/B — pays 30, and `InfluenceMeterSourceScanTests` keeps the Story cost out of `scripts/FighterSim/` and `InfluenceMeterRules.cs`. `systems_card_echo_body` no longer names a cost.
- **The V7.3 combat batch's second half landed 2026-08-26** (PRs 4–7, both modes where applicable): Rally reclaim is now `min(pool, hitDamage × BasicComboRules.RallyReclaimDamageMultiplier (2.0))` — the pool persists and keeps draining at the unchanged rate (no more one-poke full-pool cashouts; a defied hit still generates no echo, and throws collect the echo like any direct hit); Story environmental damage routes through the new `PlayerController.ApplyEnvironmentalDamage` chokepoint (extractor discharge, drown/searchlight/rift ticks, escape catches, Level 1/7 hazards, `StageHazard` — Defy-flag consumption, echo accrual, victim meter; `StageHazard` also gained a per-body float damage accumulator, fixing the `(int)(Damage*dt)` truncation that dealt zero at 60 FPS; **since 2026-10-16 a raised, front-facing block absorbs a blockable Story hazard tick for one charge** — decision 7.4, `USER-STORY-HAZARD-BLOCK`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-HAZARD-BLOCK-SOURCES`, `VERIFY-STORY-HAZARD-BLOCK` closed — through `PlayerController.ApplyEnvironmentalHit(damage, hitOrigin, blockable)` (`PlayerController.EnvironmentalBlock.cs`), whose order is the invulnerability gate (dead, rolling, post-rewind, tech or knockdown invulnerable: `Protected`, nothing spent; CM-FIXB-STORY B7), D04, Aegis and the barrier, then the block as a Hazard-class `BlockSystem.ResolveHit` with its damage-scaled shieldstun and the perfect block, then the old remainder pipeline; a block deals no HP and gives no echo or meter. The blockable sources are the cyclic hazards, the Extractor discharge (its −20 meter drain still applies, D03c), the searchlight strike, the spikes and the Collapse Tremor's falling debris; drowning, Level 7's haul-out, the rift snap, the escape catch, Level 0's scripted kill and `StageHazard` stay unblockable penalties on `ApplyEnvironmentalDamage`); the Story landing tech is a real 12-frame locked window (invulnerable, in place, no actions, then Idle — and the old `IsOnFloor() && !_wasGrounded` landing test was unsatisfiable, so Story tech had never fired at all; a per-stun airborne latch fixed it); Sudden Death disables Chronal Orbs (live orbs cleared on entry, `FighterOrbSystem` early-returns; **the "hazards stay forced on" half was superseded by V7.6 F22 — the house toggle is respected**); timeout compares HP as a percentage of max with echo pools excluded (**superseded by F21: Time is now decided by fewest stocks lost, with no HP tiebreak**), a simultaneous final-stock KO enters Sudden Death, and a dual lethal trade at 100/100 meter fires both Defy procs (sequential intent application; the second attacker legitimately re-earns meter from its own post-shatter hit); the Paris Dampening Beam drains 2.5 meter points per 30-frame tick (the doc's 5%/s — the old 5/tick was double); and mirror matches are allowed (the character-select duplicate-pick refusal and its unavailable-feedback plumbing were removed; a doubly-locked tile blends both lock colours).
- **V7.4 Enemy Stagger Discipline landed 2026-08-29 (Story-only; ends the PvE stun-lock).** Constants live in the new rulebook `FTT.Combat.EnemyStaggerRules` (`scripts/Combat/EnemyStaggerRules.cs` — plain constants, no Godot types); the behavior lives entirely in `EnemyController`. Four rules: **armored getup recovery** — a NATURALLY expiring hitstun arms `GetupArmorFrames` (36, 0.6 s) during which hits deal full damage but apply no stun and no knockback, the enemy acts freely, and the shared gold hyper-armor glow plays (a hit landing mid-stun still refreshes stun normally — the string and finisher are untouched); an **elite stagger budget** (`Tier == Elite`, which includes the Chrono-Warden) — each post-resistance applied stun accrues, decaying 1 s/s while unstunned, and exceeding 2.0 s triggers **Armored Recovery** (1.5 s flinch- and knockback-proof, budget reset, and an immediately committed signature attack through the normal `BeginAttack`/`SelectNextAttack` path — elite ability if off cooldown, else primary — so the ordinary class colour + glyph telegraph plays); **diminishing special stun** — a Special-class stun landing within 4.0 s of the previous special-sourced stun on that enemy applies full damage but stun ×0.5, window refreshed per special (basics and ultimates untouched); and the **pressure-exit rule** — stun or armored recovery ending with the target inside `AttackRangePixels` prefers `EnterAttacking` (normal reaction delay/cadence/cooldowns) over dropping to Chase. **Bosses are compliant with zero boss code**: `BossController` has no Stunned state and its hit intake ignores `HitPayload.HitstunDuration` entirely (pinned in `BossControllerTests`); `BossStaggerBudgetSeconds`/`BossArmoredRecoverySeconds` are authored for the day a flinching boss appears. **Scope guard:** nothing in `scripts/FighterSim` consumes `EnemyStaggerRules` — Fighter PvP keeps DI/tech/hit-2 escape as its whole answer (the amended No Juggling pillar). **Since 2026-10-11 (D6a / D6c, `USER-FINISHER-CARRY`, `USER-MOB-MELEE-RANGE`):** a finisher flight's pressure exit runs at touchdown, not at stun expiry; and a mob whose next attack is its capped MeleeStrike / ChargeDash primary takes the pressure exit and the Armored Recovery answer only in reach — out of reach the pressure exit falls to Chase, and the armored answer advances armored in Chase with the answer owed (`EnemyController.ArmoredAnswerOwed`), committing it the moment the target is in reach with no cooldown gate and no reaction delay, inside the window; the debt lapses with the window.
- **Story enemy combat rules (playtest feel pass 2026-10-04, workstream ENEMY and fix-round items E1–E5; Story-only, most rules provisional, ledger section "workstream ENEMY").** Every number lives in one rulebook, `FTT.Combat.StoryEnemyCombatRules` (`scripts/Combat/StoryEnemyCombatRules.cs`, no Godot types); the behaviour lives in `EnemyController`, with the shared victim-hitstop rule also read by `BossController`. Nothing in `scripts/FighterSim/` references it. **S1** a stunned mob's knockback decays with friction (1800 px/s² grounded, 600 airborne) instead of being zeroed, so the string and finisher move mobs; a ground mob's knockback heading over a drop (a 64 px probe grounded, 480 px airborne) is cancelled at the edge. A stun *opened* by a hit or call that writes no knockback — an impulse-free construct bite or coil arc, a zero-knockback Special, a direct `ApplyStun` such as a standalone Static Charge — stops the mob's horizontal motion instead, so the friction never slides chase, patrol or ChargeDash speed; a refresh of a running stun keeps the slide (fix round E1). **S2 confirm proration** (*provisional*, amended 2026-10-11 by D4, `USER-FIRST-SPECIAL-UNTAXED` / `PROVISIONAL-FIRST-SPECIAL-TAX-RULES`): **the chain's first Special lands untaxed** — identified by `AttackID` (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.ClassifyChainSpecial`, `EnemyController.ChainFirstSpecialID`; a Special-class tick may claim it, the Rift collapse rides its E=mc² execution, a movement-ability hit never does), with no ×0.6 and no chain decay on any of its hits, which still advance the chain counter; a **second or later** Special whose first hit lands on a mob already in hitstun deals ×0.6 on every hit of that Special, and within one uninterrupted stun chain each hit after the third decays ×0.85, floored at ×0.5. Ultimates, ticks and hazards are exempt and do not advance the chain, and bosses are never prorated. **S3 standard poise** (*provisional*, re-derived by D4): Standard-tier mobs get a **2.325 s** stagger budget (139.5 frames = the 102-frame string + `FirstSpecialStunAllowanceFrames` 36, the worst authored first-Special charge, + 1.5), the chain's first Special charges ×1.0 and a second or later Special ×1.5, it drains **4.0 s/s**, and a trip opens a 0.6 s Armored Recovery with the committed counterattack — so Joan's Righteous Smite → hit 1 → hit 2 → hit 3 fits (132 frames) and any second Special after it trips (≥ 141); there is no count rule (the addendum). Throws and a bowled standard's knockdown charge nothing, while a bowled elite keeps its V7.4 budget charge (E3); Ultimate-class or Ultimate-origin stuns charge no standard poise (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.ChargesStandardPoise`, E2), so a multi-hit Ultimate never trips a standard mid-cast, though the elite budget still charges them; and no poise accrues while frozen or `DrillInvulnerable`. **S4** (*provisional*) the universal basic set deals ×1.25 to Standard-tier mobs on Story intake; the shared `.tres` damages are untouched. **Since 2026-10-16 that intake also has a floor** (decision 8.1, `USER-STORY-BASIC-FLOOR`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-BASIC-FLOOR-ORDER`): `StoryEnemyCombatRules.StandardBasicPacingScale(base)` = max(1.25, `StandardBasicDamageFloor` 6 / base) replaces the bare ×1.25, before the chain and Punish scales under the one rounding (hit 4 of a chain decays a floored 6 to 5; a punished floored 6 is 7), so every hero kills a Normal `chrono_slasher` (40 HP) in 5–6 basic hits (measured; the same rounding at the old flat ×1.25 gives Leonardo, Cleopatra, Tesla and Mozart 7, derived, not measured). **Prorated numbers are dimmed** (8.2, `USER-DIM-REDUCED-NUMBERS`): a hit the ×0.6 second-Special tax or the ×0.85 chain decay reduced (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.IsProrated`) floats in `UIPalette.DamageNumberReduced` (0.66, 0.68, 0.74) at `ProratedDamageNumberScale` 0.8; an untaxed first Special and an S4 or floor increase never dim, a punished hit shows the Punish treatment, and there is no poise-trip flash. **S5** string hits 1 and 2 floor the applied stun at 34 / 44 frames, so resistant mobs are not released mid-string; the elite budget is still charged at the old 24-frame floor. **F5 victim half:** mobs and bosses take no victim hitstop from Tick, Construct or Hazard deliveries or `ExemptFromHitstop`; otherwise they freeze for the attacker's own launch-aware `BasicComboRules.HitstopFrames(dealt, launches)` (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.VictimHitstopFrames`, E4), so both parties of a launching hit freeze equally, and a lethal direct hit holds the corpse for that freeze (the kill freeze) before the death animation; the kill, dust and collision removal stay immediate. **F9:** player-dealt hits on mobs and bosses shake the camera (an authored ability `ScreenShakeIntensity` × `AuthoredShakePixelsPerIntensity`, which the Story player-hit path reads too, or a small damage/launch curve) and float damage numbers; since 2026-10-16 a non-Ultimate Special shakes by the larger of the authored value and the damage curve, intensity and duration (decision 7.1, `USER-STORY-ABILITY-SHAKE`; `StoryEnemyCombatRules.ScalesAbilityShakeWithDamage`; the Emancipator 8 px), Ultimates keep the authored shake and basics the curve, the screen-shake accessibility scale still applies, and the same rule covers the hero and the Level 13 clone hitting each other (`AttackerIndex >= 0`). **P4 chase leash** (*provisional*): an aggroed mob keeps chasing while its target is inside its room's x-bounds or within 1.5× `DeAggroRadius` (since 2026-10-12 an engaged encounter-lock member chases anywhere in its area instead, and since FIX-ENEMY an engaged melee member steps off a ledge toward a hero more than 64 px below; Scene architecture's encounter-lock bullet). In Chase it stops at drops deeper than 64 px and at Environment walls, and holds under a target directly above; standard ground mobs still never jump. Projectiles spawn from a facing-mirrored `AbilityOrigin`. A pool cycle, a rewind restore and a throw whose damage killed the mob clear the grab/throw latches and any owed Down-Air slam bounce (`EnemyController.ResetGrabAndSlamState`, E5). **D8 Punish window (2026-10-11, `USER-PUNISH-WINDOW`, `PROVISIONAL-PUNISH-WINDOW-RULES`):** a non-shatter block of a mob's attack (projectiles, Guard-Crush and a blocked Siphon Snare included), a Foresight counter of it, or its MeleeStrike / ChargeDash active window ending with nothing reaching the hero while the hero is near (its pivot reach + 40 px horizontally, 96 px vertically; a roll-through counts, a wall-blocked swing does not) opens a 60-frame window on that mob (`EnemyController.IsPunishWindowOpen`) in which the hero's player-dealt direct hits deal ×1.2 (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.PunishDamageScale`, after S2/D4 and S4, one rounding), their damage numbers drawn in `UIPalette.GoldBright` at 1.25×. It runs on the mob's world clock (paused only by freezes, not by its own hitstop), refreshes rather than stacks, and covers standard and elite mobs only — never bosses (`VERIFY-BOSS-HP` is held) or the Mirror clone. Mob payloads name their owner through `Hitbox.SourceActorIdOverride` / `EnemyProjectile.SourceActorId`; the one mob melee-reach helper is `StoryEnemyCombatRules.MeleePivotReachPixels`, with the named hero hurtbox `StoryCombatRules.HeroHurtboxWidthPixels` / `HeroHurtboxHeightPixels` (40 × 64). **D6a finisher carry (2026-10-11, `USER-FINISHER-CARRY`):** a hero's launching string finisher (Basic `combo_3`) on a Standard or (addendum) Elite mob arms a finisher flight that lands it one step — 30 px — outside that hero's own live hit-1 pivot reach (`PlayerController.StoryStringSpanPixels(0)` + the mob's hurtbox half-width), or for an authored finisher force ≥ 18 (only Lincoln's) at least the widest profile's reach + 45 px, so Lincoln still lands his mob farthest. It decelerates at `v²/(2(carry + v/120))` (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.FinisherCarryDeceleration`) **alone**, in place of S1 for the whole flight (since 2026-10-12, with static Story knockback; it was `max(S1, …)`), and a ground mob flies with its AI suspended until touchdown, behind a 120-frame post-stun cap; the user-locked 4.5× finisher is untouched. Measured after static knockback (2026-10-12): every hero lands an aggroed `chrono_slasher` 21.4–78.7 px outside its own hit-1 reach, touching down 45–53 frames after the connect (on 2026-10-11, 19–79 px and 61–76 frames; before D6a, −77 to +257); a carried mob keeps its engagement slot until touchdown. **D6c melee range (2026-10-11, `USER-MOB-MELEE-RANGE`):** a mob whose next attack (`EnemyController.PreviewNextAttack()`) is its MeleeStrike / ChargeDash primary commits only when the hero is horizontally within `min(AttackRangePixels, its box's pivot reach against the hero − 10)` (`CommitRangePixels`) and inside its box's height band, walks in while it can commit, and otherwise holds in a band just outside pushbox contact; projectile primaries, AreaPulse, elite and secondary abilities, the engage blink, Phase Skip and bosses keep `AttackRangePixels`. The 16 melee primaries now commit at 60–77 px (`chrono_slasher` 90 → 60). Since 2026-10-12 a melee mob without an engagement slot holds on the director's wait ring instead of walking in (the next bullet). **D9 wall bounce (2026-10-11, `USER-STORY-WALL-BOUNCE`):** a player-launched, still-stunned, non-phasing Standard or Elite mob — or (addendum) a thrown one, `LaunchThrown(velocity, bowling, throwDamage)` — that meets an Environment wall faster than 300 px/s horizontally rebounds at −40 % of that speed and takes 10 % of the launching hit's pre-defense intake (for a throw, of its damage) once, rounded half away from zero, at least 1, with no stun, hitstop, poise, meter, Rally or Punish effect; never bosses, the Mirror clone, the hero or the Fighter sim. Agent-chosen details of D6 and D9: `PROVISIONAL-SPACING-WALLS-RULES`. Suites: `StoryEnemyCombatRulesTests`, `StoryEnemyCombatTests`, `StoryEnemyCombatReviewTests`, `EnemyControllerTests`, `StoryFirstSpecialTaxTests`, `StoryPunishWindowTests`, `StoryFinisherCarryTests`, `StoryWallBounceTests`, and the D6c roster pin in `EnemyRosterContentTests`.
- **The Story engagement director — enemy turn-taking (gameplay update 2026-10-12, user request 6; ledger `USER-ENEMY-TURN-TAKING`, rules `PROVISIONAL-ENGAGEMENT-DIRECTOR-RULES`; Story only).** Only a few mobs may commit to melee on the hero at once: `StoryEngagementRules.MeleeSlotsTotal` **Easy 1 / Normal 2 / Hard 3** (the user's numbers), at most `MeleeSlotsPerSide` 1 / 1 / 2 per side. The rest hold on a **wait ring** on their own side, the nearest at `WaitRingRankZeroGapPixels` 200 / 170 / 150 px pivot to pivot and each further rank 64 px out (±12), following at min(mob speed × 0.8, 0.9 × the hero's run speed) and backing off at min(× 0.6, 0.9 ×), stopping at ledges and walls either way. `RangedPermits` 1 / 2 / 3 caps concurrent ranged attacks (the shooter refused longest fires next), and a projectile mob holds no farther than `RangedHoldMaxPixels` 560 and releases at `RangedHoldReleaseMaxPixels` 600 (the 1.5 zoom's 640 px half view, `ZoomedHalfViewPixels`, less 80 / 40; only `infrared_border_sentry` changes). **Priority** (`ComparePriority`, since FIX-F): rest elapsed → elite → the side with fewer holders → waited frames → a 32 px gap bucket → instance id. A holder hands its slot to a rested, eligible waiter when its attack ends (it then rests `RestFramesAfterHandoff` 90 / 45 / 20 own ticks before it can be handed one again), keeps it through a stun, a grab, a thrown flight or a freeze, hands it off at a finisher's touchdown only to an eligible waiter, and gives it up after `StaleSlotFrames` 120 holding without committing while someone waits. A waiter of rank 1 or more whose place is cut off by a wall, ledge or barrier crosses to the less crowded side with its pushbox off (`VERIFY-ENGAGEMENT-CROSSING-PRESENTATION`). The engage blink is granted a slot on the side it lands on (`EnemyAbilityExecutor.TeleportLandingSide`). **Gate decisions, not actions:** only `ProcessChase`'s commit and `TryEngageBlink` are gated; `BeginAttack`, `CommitArmoredAnswer`, `TryPressureExitAttack` and `TryReactivePhaseSkip` never are, so a struck waiter still retaliates in reach (V7.4) — the one way the bots see more melee attackers near the hero than slots, which their telemetry now separates (`frames_gated_melee_over_slots`). A lone mob behaves frame-identically to before. The registry (`StoryEngagementDirector`: static, per target, pruned on every access, slot counts recomputed from the members' own flags, no clock of its own) serves only the campaign hero: `EnemyController.FindNearestPlayer` and `BossController.FindNearestPlayer` skip `IsStoryHostile`, so nothing targets the Level 13 Mirror clone; bosses never hold a slot, their summons do; dormant lock members never join, and a lock's engage joins all its members at once. Code: `scripts/Combat/StoryEngagementRules.cs` (pure, difficulty a parameter), `scripts/Enemies/StoryEngagementDirector.cs`, `scripts/Enemies/EnemyController.Engagement.cs`. Mob-to-mob pushbox jostling is deferred (`DEFER-MOB-PUSHBOX-JOSTLE`), and waiters never strike first. Suites: `StoryEngagementRulesTests` (9, pure C#), `StoryEngagementDirectorTests` (19, including the source scan `NothingInTheFighterSimNamesTheEngagementDirector`).
- **V7.4 CPU hitstun defense landed 2026-08-29 (Fighter Mode, input-side only — no combat-rules change).** `FighterCpuController` no longer zeroes every input during hitstun: `CpuBandTuning` gains `HitstunDefensePercent`/`DiPercent` (Easy 10/0, Normal 45/40, Hard 85/80 per the design §10 Hitstun Defense rows), rolled **once per hitstun instance** (detected as `HitstunFrames` rising from zero or above the previous frame's value — a mid-stun refresh re-rolls). A successful defense roll holds Block — **exclusively**, so a scheduled attack edge can never read as the grab chord and drop the shield — through the hitstun, the tumble, ground contact, and a 45-frame grounded linger (`EscapeStanceLingerFrames`), buying the hit-2 escape, the blocked rest of the string, and the landing tech through the ordinary sim rules; a successful DI roll holds the stick toward safety during the launch window. The rolls ride the same recorded-input path as the existing BlockPercent draws, so rollback replays them; the daze branch still returns None. The full constructor gained an optional `CpuBandTuning? tuningOverride` test seam. `FighterCpuHitstunDefenseTests` pins the matrix, the roll gates, and both sim integrations. **Since 2026-10-16 the hold is exclusive only during the hitstun and the grounded linger** (CM-FIXB-CPU B0 / B20, `FIX-CPU-HITSTUN-HOLD-EATS-RECOVERY`, `PROVISIONAL-CPU-HITSTUN-HOLD-PASS-THROUGH`): once the hitstun is over and the CPU is airborne or hanging, it holds Block plus the planner's own Jump and movement-ability presses (and the jump sustain), still masking BasicAttack and Roll, so a defended CPU whose hitstun ends over a pit recovers instead of falling into the blast zone, and one the ledge catches climb-jumps. A recovery movement activation is charged to the episode when it goes out, not when it is delivered.
- **V7.4's "pit-aware DI is deliberately not implemented" sentence is retired.** Package 11 A9b ships it: `ResolveDiHold` aims at the **nearest pit-facing floor edge** while the launch trajectory ends over a gap, and keeps the unchanged toward-centre hold everywhere else. On Paris that is the difference between DI and suicide, because stage centre is the hole.
- **V7.6 F19 CPU recovery and verb policy landed 2026-09-13 (Package 11 A9b, Fighter Mode, input-side only).** The inverted percentage ladder is replaced by a tiered planner: **Easy** steers at the nearest legal landing, spends its jumps, and gets **one** movement-ability activation per offstage episode with **no Specials anywhere**; **Medium** estimates reachability with the jumps in hand and otherwise spends one suitable ability, the movement ability by default, also capped at one per episode; **Hard** compares routes, chains where the kit actually refunds, and past `OffstageStallLimitFrames` (240) drops every optional hesitation so it cannot stall offstage. The **offstage episode latch** is input-side and outside the rollback snapshot, and ends only on a stable landing on *authored* stage geometry, a legal ledge capture, or a KO — so a jump refund or a temporary staff platform does not reset the cap. The activation is charged **when the press goes out, not at decision**, because the 128-slot schedule ring can overwrite a pending slot under Easy's jitter (it was charged at delivery until 2026-10-16; since CM-FIXB-CPU it is charged at emission, so a press the hitstun hold masks or a later delivery overwrites costs nothing, `FIX-CPU-HITSTUN-HOLD-EATS-RECOVERY`). Recovery detection is "unsupported over a gap", reachable only on Open stages (`HasFloorSegments == 0` on a Sealed stage), so closed stages cannot trigger a false recovery — no Y threshold anywhere. `CpuRecoveryProfile` holds the nine per-character plans and is **built from the CPU's own normalized `FighterLoadout`**; it is code-owned like `CpuBandTuning` and **must never become a resource**, because Fighter Mode stays competitively normalized. `RecoverySpecialTwoPercent` is retired in favour of `RecoveryMobilitySpecialPercent`. **No character approves a mobility Special since the Tubman swap** (Package 13 W5): Pocahontas's Spirit Strike, the one Package 12 W4 approved, left with her, so `CpuRecoveryProfile.MobilitySpecialFor` returns `None` for the whole roster and the planner branch and `RecoveryMobilitySpecialPercent` are dormant; `CPU_RECOVERY.md` says Tubman's Specials are not recovery tools. Tubman plans North Star Leap as a directional move with its 0.5-unit ledge-snap bonus (`CpuRecoveryProfile.MovementLedgeSnapBonus`); W7a/W7b added `MovementKindGust = 6`, `MovementKindWingDive = 10` and `MovementKindSandRush = 11`, and recovery reach is recomputed from the loadouts. Easy casts no Special anywhere. The CPU also uses **grabs and Echo Step**, emitted as the shared chords a human presses: Easy neither, Medium 25 % admission for both, Hard scored (a grab must actually improve position; Echo Step weighs the 30-meter spend against a viable Ultimate setup and an unused eligible Defy). Admission is rolled **once per opportunity** and a failed roll never reopens a continuing one. **Since 2026-10-08 a recovery jump is revalidated at delivery** (`ScheduledDecision.RecoveryJump`; `PROVISIONAL-MOVEMENT-FEEL-RULES` item 9): one arriving after the CPU's own jumps are spent loses its Jump bit and keeps its steer, so jumps queued before the first press was observed no longer overwrite Medium's one pending movement-ability edge — no RNG draw, no snapshot state. The pit-risk probe predicts the apex hang (`Observe(..., maxJumpCount)`). **Since 2026-10-16 the CPU holds Jump for a full hop** (decision 1.1(5), `FIX-CPU-JUMP-HOLD`): a pulse carrying Jump hands its hold to an input-side sustain (`_jumpSustained`) that ends at `JumpSustainReleaseVelocityRaw` (−2 u/s, just past the apex-hang band), on landing, in hitstun or daze, with no stocks, when the hitstun-defense hold takes over, or after `JumpSustainMaxFrames` (45); a queued second jump first releases Jump for one tick (`EdgeReleaseGapFrames`), so it arrives one tick later. The Level 13 clone's ground jump now peaks at 186.4 px, the height of a held single jump (before, its whole two-press approach arc peaked at 131.4 px). The recovery planner's movement and jump presses wait for the CPU's own Special cast and are dropped uncharged if delivered inside it (`FIX-CPU-RECOVERY-PRESS-IN-CAST`); Hard's Echo Step no longer reserves meter for a Defy that Sudden Death bars (`FIX-CPU-DEFY-RESERVE-IN-SUDDEN-DEATH`); and a hanging CPU keeps its DI on a hit's thaw tick (`FIX-CPU-HANG-DI-ZEROED`). Four wasted or wrongly charged presses remain (`VERIFY-CPU-DELIVERY-RESIDUALS`). Still open: jumps queued inside one reaction window can spend the second jump mid-rise, two edges inside one 3-tick pulse overwrite each other through `_pendingEdges`, and every recovery input carries the full reaction delay.
- **The CPU's BasicAttack reads a per-fighter melee band (2026-10-11, input-side only; ledger `FIX-CPU-REACH-BLIND-OPENER`, agent-chosen details `PROVISIONAL-CPU-MELEE-BAND`).** Inside the 2.0-unit `CloseRange` the CPU used to press its opener whatever its reach, so a CPU Lincoln or Joan whiffed from the edge of close range. Now the BasicAttack fallback reads `FighterCpuController.MeleeBand(reach, step, targetHalf)`: the far edge of the string hit the press would start (from `BasicComboRules`' string table × `ReachWidthPercent`), plus the target's hurtbox half-width, minus `MeleeBandMarginUnits` (0.1). The step is `CpuDecisionObservation.SelfNextStringStep` (`FighterBasicAttackRules.NextChainStep` in the sim, `PlayerController.NextChainStepForPress` for the Mirror), so a press during the finisher or a directional swing reads the opener's band.
