### What Package 13 did NOT deliver (2026-09-30)

Assembled from the eight workstream handoffs under `docs/handoffs/P13_*.md` and the Phase C closeout; the full record is `docs/PACKAGE13_REVIEW_ALIGNMENT_PLAN.md` §10 and the ledger (`docs/design-contracts/DESIGN_BUILD_DEVIATIONS.md`, whose new Package 13 table holds 22 rows).

**Nobody has played any of it, looked at it, or balanced it.** No reworked kit, air dodge, Down-Air slam, activation strike, Tubman match, Level 0 hold, hub visit, rewritten script, Hakata Bay run, send-off or Homecoming was played or seen by a person. Every acceptance claim above a unit test is reasoned from shipped code. Every new number is the reviews' provisional (A07) value or an agent's placeholder (`VERIFY-P13-PROVISIONAL-VALUES`, `VERIFY-TUBMAN-PROVISIONAL`, `VERIFY-ULT-ACTIVATION-TUNING`), and every new surface — Tubman's art, the watcher, the triage readout, Captain Smith's taking, the near-capture, the send-off and Homecoming stills — is placeholder presentation. Audio is still digital silence.

**Still held for a user ruling** (carried from Package 12): `VERIFY-BOSS-HP` (the ten unreconciled Act II/III rows), `VERIFY-STORY-PITS` (zero lethal boundaries; the 4A option lapsed), `VERIFY-ERASER-DUST` (now read only outside Level 5's ledger), `VERIFY-PARIS-DEEP-PIT-RECOVERY` (its matrix re-run green on 2026-10-16; the sim's any-depth floor snap it may lean on is `VERIFY-SIM-PIT-FLOOR-SNAP`), `DEFER-CPU-SNAPSHOT`, `VERIFY-L15-STANDIN-PLACEMENT`. `VERIFY-STORY-HAZARD-BLOCK` was ruled on 2026-10-16 (decision 7.4, a raised block absorbs a Story hazard tick) and is closed.

**New questions for the user**, each with a ledger row: `VERIFY-WING-DIVE-LEAP`, `VERIFY-ULT-CINEMATIC-PAUSE`, `VERIFY-ULT-FINALE-ORDER`, `VERIFY-STORY-MIRROR-HOLD`, `VERIFY-NEAR-CAPTURE-PLACEMENT`, `VERIFY-ENEMY-PUSHBOX-WIDTH`, `VERIFY-P13-AUTHORED-COPY`, and the design master's binding-rename defect (`DOC-MASTER-UNBOUND-BINDING`). Still open from Package 12: `VERIFY-ABILITY-LAUNCHES` (partly ruled by the character review), `VERIFY-BOSS-PHASE-ARENAS`, `VERIFY-BOSS-TABLE-DRIFT`, `DEFER-DRONE-CARRIER`, `DEFER-FIGHTER-SAND-DECOY`, `VERIFY-M05-KNOCKDOWN-SCOPE`, `VERIFY-P12-PROVISIONAL-VALUES`.

Open, by area:

- **Combat.** Sim Specials fired on press until 2026-10-16 (`DEFER-SIM-SPECIAL-PHASES`, plan D9, closed by decision 1.2: every authored startup / active / recovery now applies in both modes, the Combat section's Fighter Special bullet). Ultimates still use the flattened knockback scalar (`DEFER-ULT-KNOCKBACK-SCALAR`). Conductor's Call and Requiem Chord use local stand-ins for the shared ground-wave and burst primitives (`DEFER-GROUND-WAVE-UNIFY`, `DEFER-REQUIEM-BURST-UNIFY`). The sim's hit hurtbox is still 1.0 wide (`VERIFY-SIM-HURTBOX-WIDTH`); an owed slam bounce can carry over to a second launch (`VERIFY-SLAM-BOUNCE-CARRYOVER`); the sim Tempest does not skip an Ultimate-held victim (`VERIFY-TEMPEST-ULT-HOLD`); Story Rail Charge armor covers its startup (`VERIFY-RAIL-CHARGE-STARTUP-ARMOR`); `FighterLoadoutFactory` still folds multi-hit Special totals into the tuning component (`VERIFY-TUNING-SPECIAL-TOTAL-FOLD`; since 2026-10-16 only single-hit Splitting Strike reaches the folded generic melee branch, while the CPU and the Move List still read the folded totals). The bespoke per-kit sim hitstun constants that remain are `DEFER-SIM-ABILITY-HITSTUN`, and the Ultimate finale uses a shared 30-frame hitstun rather than the authored one. The Move List has no dedicated air-dodge row. Bosses ignore the sand decoy, and there is no Extra Note charge widget. Since 2026-10-08 the normal-control terminal is shared at 12 u/s (`USER-MOVEMENT-FEEL`); the launch terminals (Story 10 u/s, sim 20) still differ until H-7 (`DEFER-H7-STORY-TERMINAL`, narrowed; the per-mode base gravities are intended since the 2026-10-09 ruling, and its short-hop half closed on 2026-10-16), and Story's aerial Specials do not fast-fall — since 2026-10-16 neither do the sim's, so that half of `VERIFY-MOVEMENT-FEEL-FALLOUT` is parity. In Toggle Block mode the Echo Step chord is unreachable from a latch. Grounded knockback has no friction during hitstun (an F09 question).
- **Ultimates.** The activation wind-up's red tint and X glyph shipped on 2026-10-16 (`DEFER-ULT-TELEGRAPH`, closed); `ActivationHitboxOffset` / `ActivationProjectileScene` were not added; the world does not pause during a cinematic; Cleopatra's slam closes rather than opens her sequence; the Mirror clone's Story cinematic does not hold the player.
- **Campaign and narrative.** Level 0 lacks beat 1's per-hero lines and stills, beat 7's hazards, visored Linked locals and Wren's replay of every lesson (`DEFER-LEVEL0-BEATS`). The hub's act-boundary **portal gate** is still not enforced (`DEFER-HUB-PORTAL-GATE`), and the hub is still one corridor. The S44 lesser returns are narration lines, not ~1 s in-engine beats; the captive-return montage is still token lines. The Level 9 near-capture sits in room 3, not the night stealth; the Level 9 boss arena half is still unbuilt. Level 5's `ParSeconds` (270) was not reseeded for the added Eraser debut (`VERIFY-PAR-SECONDS`, still entirely seeded). `epilogue_dialogue.tres` has no content-manifest row. `speaker_player` still reads "Traveler" as the slotless fallback. The Levels 1–4 route-reachability audit under the locked kit was never run. N01 presentation is placeholder (`VERIFY-N01-PRESENTATION`). Since 2026-10-11 the results itemisation shows held dust carried across a Collapse resume on its own line (`PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST`); `PressurePlate` is still not freezable. Two code comments still say "Tidal Eraser".
- **Fighter flow.** The hazard cadence is 2700 frames on all ten stages, seeded and untuned. There is no Random stage "Open-only / Sealed-only" option. Remote Play is a notice (`DEFER-STEAM-RPT`). The Fighter driver has no animation mapping for the new kit phases (a lunge reads as `run`, the rush/glissando/dive as `jump`/`fall`), draws no warp ghost or gust pose, and E=mc²'s rotating item is not built.
- **Front end and accessibility.** The Steam overlay, Cloud and rich presence are no-op interfaces; crash reports are local only; no font asset ships; the pseudo-locale screen sweep is `VERIFY-PSEUDO-LOCALE-SCREENS`; the Dialogue Log action has no Controls row; the Settings tab order differs from the GDD.
- **Presentation and audio.** Story grab/throw/Echo Step/tech/knockdown cues have no `EventBus` publisher and nothing raises `OnRallyEchoChanged`. No per-hero victory fanfares; the Time Freeze drone is a one-shot; the KO music drop and the Defy duck are not implemented; the Collapse Tremor's decorative half and audio are missing. A `HubWorld.tscn` / `Level_00_Tutorial.tscn --quit-after 300` smoke run reports 6 leaked ObjectDB instances and 3 resources in use at exit against 2 / 1 for control levels (not compared against a pre-Package-13 build; see CLAUDE.md on that noise).
- **Architecture.** `DEFER-SAVE-VERSION-SPLIT` (v8 was again a shared bump), `DEFER-ROSTER-ENUM` (exercised once by the swap), `DEFER-M04-SUBPHASE-FLAGS` and `DEFER-VARIANT-KEY-SEPARATOR` stand. `IStatusEffectTarget` coexists with `IDamageable` by design. The save-state-dependent `Handle is not initialized` interop fault on hub boot is still not fixed.
- **Out of scope by decision:** Steamworks, Package 7 native online/LAN, par measurement and V01/F09/V02/S01 validation, platform and hardware runs, balance, every Package 10 art/audio half, H-7 gravity unification (its short-hop half closed on 2026-10-16), the M-18 drop-through divergence (closed on 2026-10-16), achievements and advanced Training Mode.

### The 2026-10-11 code-research fix batch (committed as `2e9ca79` on 2026-10-12)

Eight fix steps from a code-research pass, each reviewed, landed in the working tree on 2026-10-11; the ledger has one section per step (titles dated 2026-10-11) and the bullets above carry the detail. In short: the default gamepad layout moved to Godot 4 button numbers with a load-time repair for stale override rows (Autoloads, `InputManager`); fresh level entry and every full Restart reset HP and meter, Quit to Main Menu flushes and keeps its fee, and a reloaded Game Over restarts the right level (Story progression, the F10 bullet); the Influence meter's numbers live once in `InfluenceMeterRules` and Mozart's build rate is wired (Combat); three dead or inverted grid nodes were repaired and the dormant gate turned on, and purchases and respecs now apply to the live hero (Story progression, the grid bullet); the grounded string boxes live once in `BasicComboRules` and the AttackRange minors move the far edge by their full percentage; the CPU, the Mirror and the bots read a reach-aware melee band, and the finisher bar is per kit with the 2.5-unit floor kept; and the Story drop buff reaches every Special, Ultimate, construct and zone tick through one damage composition. **Nobody has played or looked at any of it**; every acceptance claim is headless. Still open from it, each with a ledger row: `VERIFY-GAMEPAD-ULTIMATE-CHORD` (a) and (c) (both closed on 2026-10-16 by decision 9.2's chord leniency), `PROVISIONAL-GAMEPAD-OVERRIDE-NO-MIGRATION` (Time Freeze's button was picked later that day: L3, D11), `PROVISIONAL-F10-FRESH-HERO-PENDING`, `VERIFY-HUB-QUIT-EXIT-FEE`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-METER-GAIN-SCOPE`, `PROVISIONAL-GRID-DORMANT-GATE-SOURCE`, `VERIFY-GRID-DORMANT-NAME-IN-PREREQ-TEXT`, `PROVISIONAL-GRID-LIVE-APPLY`, `PROVISIONAL-REACH-MINOR-SHAPE`, five of the six string-geometry `VERIFY-*` rows (the cross-mode parity bullet; the aerial one was closed by D6b), `PROVISIONAL-CPU-MELEE-BAND` and `PROVISIONAL-STORY-ABILITY-DAMAGE-ROUTING`. The batch's finalize step also fixed the `MainMenuSceneTests` session leak (coding conventions; CLAUDE.md failure signature 8) and three stale comments (the lockout's same-tick order in `BasicComboRules`, the pause menu's exit fee, and the hub deposit `ResonanceProgression.SpendableBalance` described).

### The 2026-10-11 combat-flow pass (committed as `2e9ca79` on 2026-10-12)

The user's combat-flow rulings of 2026-10-11 — the goal, in their words, "more synergy between standard attacks and specials" — were built in the working tree on 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-12, after the code-research batch, by five reviewed spec groups plus the bot telemetry. The rulings (D1–D13 and an addendum) live outside the repository in `D:/Projects/absolum_research/DECISIONS.md`; the ledger's "2026-10-11 user rulings" section indexes every one to its user row and every agent choice to its `PROVISIONAL-*` row, and the bullets above carry the detail. In short: a landed basic swing cancels straight into Special 1 or 2, while a whiff and Special → Special keep the 30-frame lockout and the Ultimate keeps it after every swing (D1 / D1a, both modes, with a freeze-only Special buffer); every landed swing refunds both Special cooldowns (D2, per-hit Momentum, bosses included); a press the lockout alone refused flashes its HUD slot (D10, both modes); and, in Story only, a landed Special's recovery cancels into a roll, a jump, any basic variant or the movement ability (D3), the first Special in a mob's stun chain lands untaxed (D4), a non-shatter block, a Foresight counter or a mob's nearby whiff opens a 60-frame ×1.2 Punish window on that mob (D8), the campaign hero earns meter ×0.35 (D5), aerial strings reach as far as the grounded string (D6b), a finisher lands its mob one step outside the hero's hit-1 reach (D6a), melee mobs commit only inside their own reach (D6c), and a launched or thrown mob bounces off walls (D9). The gamepad Time Freeze moved to L3 (D11); mob dust no longer expires and completion banks every pickup still on the ground, with a results itemisation that now adds up across reloads (D12); every campaign level places one Restoration Font (D13); and the bots report swings, finishers, meter, refused presses and mob swings (D6d). No Klotho component ID was allocated (305's unread `AegisHits` int and 310's Momentum counters were reused in place), and no save field, schema or protocol changed. **Nobody has played or looked at any of it** — not the flash, the fonts, the aerial poses, the walk-back after a finisher, the wall bounce or L3 on a pad; every acceptance claim is headless or a bot batch. The bots' before / after measurement (`D:/Projects/absolum_research/flow/bots_before.md` and `bots_after.md`, 144 runs each, seeds 0–3 pooled) found one result that needs a ruling, `VERIFY-COMBAT-FLOW-BURST-KILLS` (Story standards now die within 2 s of the first hit far more often: Normal `play` 5 → 22 %, Joan under the `confirm` press policy 64 %), and one feel risk for the hands-on pass: the first swing after a finisher whiffs more often (Normal `play` next-swing connect 94.6 → 87.8 %), nearly always a grounded hit 1 about 30 px short — exactly the step D6a lands the mob at. Still open from it, each with a ledger row: every user row's hands-on acceptance and its design-master amendment (the index section lists the lines owed); the agent choices in `PROVISIONAL-HIT-CONFIRM-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-FREEZE-SPECIAL-BUFFER`, `PROVISIONAL-FIRST-SPECIAL-TAX-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-PUNISH-WINDOW-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-SPECIAL-RECOVERY-CANCEL-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-METER-SCALE-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-SPACING-WALLS-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-DUST-SETTLE-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST` and `PROVISIONAL-FONT-PLACEMENT`; `VERIFY-STORY-INVULN-LAYER-ORDER`, `VERIFY-PENDING-MOB-PICKUP-RECOVERY`, `VERIFY-FONT-PENDING-HEAL-RESUME`, `VERIFY-CRITICAL-COMMIT-WALLET` and `DEFER-CHRONAL-FEAST-PLACEMENT`; and `USER-COMBAT-FLOW-NOT-NOW`, what the rulings deliberately left out (a heavy button, Special clash, a visible poise break, elite HP cuts, closing `DEFER-SIM-SPECIAL-PHASES` — which is why D3 was Story-only — and more; the 2026-10-16 combat and movement update reopened that item and closed it, so D3 is now one rule in both modes). Two bot-side problems are not game bugs: on Easy Level 11 the `play` bot sometimes breaks ShieldArrayBravo first and then strikes Alpha from the floor, so those runs take about 70 s longer (a `PlaytestBots` targeting fix was owed; since FIX-BOT, 2026-10-13, the bot strikes a breakable from beside its base inside an engaged lock, `PrefersBeside`, and Alpha breaks about 50 s sooner); and the telemetry resolves a press on the next tick, so a Special press the freeze buffer fires after the freeze is booked as refused under `hitstop` (`USER-BOT-COMBAT-TELEMETRY` lists this and its other limits).

### The 2026-10-12 gameplay update

The user's six requests of 2026-10-12 — a single aerial attack, a closer camera, beat-'em-up areas to clear, movement −20 %, static Story knockback and enemies that wait their turn — with four scope rulings (both modes for the aerial strike and the speed cut; every area with enemies plus every puzzle room that blocks the way forward, across all levels; Easy 1 / Normal 2 / Hard 3 attackers at once). Planned in `docs/GAMEPLAY_UPDATE_2026-10-12_PLAN.md` and built on 2026-10-12 and 2026-10-13 by six Phase A workstreams, a Phase A review fix round, five Phase B workstreams and a Phase C fix round, each merged into `main` (from the plan commit `b8f5343` to `681a525`, then the closeout's Level 7 ramp fix `4ca4280`); the handoffs are `docs/handoffs/GU_*.md`, and the ledger section "Confirmed open deviations — gameplay update, 2026-10-12" with its index holds every row. In short: an airborne neutral press is one air strike that never chains, in both modes (Combat); the Story camera runs at 1.5 and frames a boss or Mirror arena (Scene architecture); every area with enemies and every progress-blocking puzzle room is one of 48 encounter locks — barriers, dormant warded members, a stall watchdog, a checkpoint hold, a one-way objective latch, a HUD counter and GO prompt, F10 baselines (Scene architecture); the nine run speeds are × 0.8 and the roll keeps its distance, in both modes (Gameplay architecture); Story knockback is static while the Fighter keeps the HP ramp (Combat); and an engagement director gives Easy 1 / Normal 2 / Hard 3 melee slots and a wait ring (Combat). Every timed level's par is scaled × 1.5 meanwhile. The bots are lock-aware and report the locks and the crowd (CLAUDE.md). No save schema, Klotho component or protocol changed. **Nobody has played or looked at any of it** — not the air strike, the zoom, the boss framing ease, a single lock fight, the barriers, the spawn tears, the GO prompt, the slower movement, the static launches, or a waiter's ring or crossing; every acceptance claim is a headless suite, a smoke run or a bot run. Still open, each with a ledger row: every user row's hands-on acceptance and its design-master amendment (the index lists the lines owed, and `HUD_CONTRACT.md` owes the lock panel); the agent choices in `PROVISIONAL-AERIAL-STRIKE-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-CAMERA-ZOOM-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-SPEED-CUT-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-STATIC-KNOCKBACK-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-ENCOUNTER-LOCK-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-LEVEL-AUTHORING`, `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-MEMBER-REACH`, `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-OBJECTIVE-LATCH`, `PROVISIONAL-ANCHOR-APPROACH`, `PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-ENGAGEMENT-DIRECTOR-RULES` and `PROVISIONAL-BOT-ENCOUNTER-RULES`; `VERIFY-PAR-SECONDS` (the × 1.5 scale is a guess; the Phase C batch's completed runs keep at least 66 % Integrity at the PreBoss lock, so it stays provisional until a human pass), `VERIFY-L07-ROPE-OVERSHOOT-BOARDING-ENGAGE`, `VERIFY-LOCK-PUZZLE-PROPS-REWIND`, `VERIFY-SCRATCH-SAVE-ENTRY-REPARK`, `VERIFY-ENGAGEMENT-CROSSING-PRESENTATION` and `DEFER-MOB-PUSHBOX-JOSTLE`. Closed by the update: `FIX-L15-ESCAPE-NEVER-COMPLETED` (both escapes now complete for a walking hero), `VERIFY-L07-ARENA-RAMP-RISERS` (Level 7's flagship ramps meet their decks through the shared `StoryLevelControllerBase.BuildRampBetweenJoints`, `4ca4280`), `VERIFY-L15-ESCAPE-REWIND-HALT` and `FIX-LOCK-SURFACED-CONTENT` (the Level 7 Eraser over open water, the mid-air Level 13 Erasers, members inside boss arenas, posted or never-aggroing members, Level 8's open tomb-corridor end, Level 5's listing ramps). Recorded limits worth knowing before a play session: at 1.5 ranged mobs can fire from beyond the half view with no off-screen indicator, room-name labels and three high Extractors are off-screen, and the Mirror clone starts off-screen for the reveal's first ~0.6 s; waiters never strike first and pass through each other; 17 mob types now outrun Lincoln; a member stalled 10 s is moved visibly; Levels 4, 6 and 8 still restore their puzzle props cosmetically on a rewind after the solve; an anchor a hero runs past is held until the next lock clears; the bots fight waiters as an aggressive player would, so their crowd numbers are an upper bound; and bot batches from before and after the update are not comparable.

### The 2026-10-16 combat and movement update

The user's combat and movement decisions of 2026-10-15 and 2026-10-16 — ten rounds and three follow-ups, from a genre review of platformers and fighting games (`D:/Projects/genre_review_2026-10-13/DECISIONS_combat_movement.md`, drawn from its `REPORT_1_PLATFORMERS.md` and `REPORT_2_FIGHTERS.md`) — and the eight plan questions Q1–Q8 ruled on 2026-10-16 (Q1, Q5, Q6 and Q7 against the drafted default). Planned in `docs/COMBAT_MOVEMENT_UPDATE_2026-10-16_PLAN.md` (plan commit `735296f`) and built on 2026-10-16 by five Phase A workstreams (CM-BOT, CM-STORY, CM-ULT, CM-INPUT and CM-SIM, whose foundation is tagged `cm-sim-a0`), a Phase A review fix round (CM-FIXA-SIM, CM-FIXA-STORY, CM-FIXA-INPUT, CM-FIXA-PRES), five Phase B workstreams (CM-HIT, CM-BLOCK, CM-MOVE, CM-CANCEL, and CM-PADUI, which the orchestrator added after the Phase A input round found that no pad could press a GUI button on Godot 4.7.1), two orchestrator merge fix-ups (`85ce072`, `fa18442`) and a Phase B review fix round (CM-FIXB-SIM, CM-FIXB-CPU, CM-FIXB-STORY, CM-FIXB-PADUI), each merged into `main` (from `cfba295` to `14374d7`). The handoffs are `docs/handoffs/CM_*.md` — where a fix-round handoff differs from the handoff it reviews, the fix round's is the current fact — and the ledger section "Confirmed open deviations — combat and movement update, 2026-10-16" with its index holds every row. In short: Fighter Specials run their authored startup, active and recovery on the one new Klotho component, **323**, with a cast lock, a brake to a stop on the ground and the air drift kept in the air (Q1, both modes), half gravity for an aerial cast, and every accepted press spending (Combat); D3 is one rule in both modes, and the CPU and the Mirror read their own cast (Combat); the Ultimate wind-up is telegraphed in Fighter and on the Mirror, the sim refuses an Ultimate from the block stance, and Story Ultimates are armoured for their startup (Combat); both modes gained a perfect block, damage-scaled shieldstun and a tumble that outlives hitstun, Defy protection ends on the survivor's first attack, the air dodge refreshes when hit, and Fighter respawn protection shrinks from 120 to 60 frames and ends on the first attack (Combat, and the match-flow bullet above); the sim gained the short hop, both modes the held-Jump hang, corner forgiveness (Story's nudge and lip pop, the sim's one-way lip pop) and both drop-through inputs, and Fighter the ledge get-ups (Gameplay architecture); Story movement abilities fall under gravity (Leonardo's glide sinks, the Rail Charge stops at 300 px), and Story gained ability shake by damage, a 10.5 Echo Step, hazard ticks absorbed by a raised block, a basic floor of 6 against standards and dimmed prorated numbers (Combat, Ability implementation); each mode states its hurtbox once, and the sim latches its box facing at active start; player 1 follows the last-used device, Local Versus is press-to-join, the pad Ultimate is the C01c recipe chord with a 3-frame leniency, and pad A / B confirm and back out of every menu (Autoloads, Scene architecture, UI); and the bots count cross-source re-hits and model the new flights (CLAUDE.md). No save schema or protocol changed (v8, v4); every Fighter hash moved. The rollback re-measure reads a worst-case 0.084 ms median / 0.168 ms p95 against the 8 ms budget, the largest snapshot 4473 B, no breach (`docs/PERFORMANCE_BASELINE.md`). In the before / after bot batches (`cmbefore*` / `cmafter*`, CLAUDE.md) deaths and damage taken per run fell on both difficulties with non-overlapping per-seed ranges (Normal deaths 4.05 → 3.00 a run, damage 450.4 → 377.6), mostly hazard and Extractor damage while enemy damage stayed flat, which fits the hazard block; the completions rose (Easy 17 → 19, Normal 9 → 12 of 20) inside overlapping ranges, so that rise is not shown.

**Nobody has played or looked at any of it** — not a Special's startup, whiff or brake, the telegraph, a perfect block or its flash, a shieldstun length, a tumble, a short hop, a nudge or a pop, a double tap, a ledge get-up, the device follow, a join prompt, a pad menu, the chord leniency or any Story feel change; every acceptance claim is a headless suite, a smoke run, a probe or a bot run. The plan's §5 is the checklist for the first two-player pad session, which decides 2.2 (Ultimate damage), 3.3 (walls), 5.4 (pace), 8.3 (hitstop), 10.2–10.4 and the feel of everything above (`USER-COMBAT-MOVEMENT-JUDGE-BY-HAND`). Still open, each with a ledger row: every user row's hands-on acceptance and its design-master amendment (the ledger index lists the lines each ruling requires, and one of them, the Controls table's Ultimate row (`:2286` at `e2c655a`, `:2320` now), is a `DOC-MASTER-UNBOUND-BINDING` line edited by hand in each copy); the agent choices in `PROVISIONAL-SIM-SPECIAL-PHASE-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-FIGHTER-SPECIAL-RECOVERY-CANCEL`, `PROVISIONAL-CPU-SPECIAL-TIMING`, `PROVISIONAL-CPU-HITSTUN-HOLD-PASS-THROUGH`, `PROVISIONAL-ULT-TELEGRAPH-LOOK`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-ULTIMATE-ARMOR`, `PROVISIONAL-TUMBLE-EXIT-ACTIONS`, `PROVISIONAL-PERFECT-BLOCK-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-SHIELDSTUN-CURVE`, `PROVISIONAL-RESPAWN-PROTECTION-CURVE`, `PROVISIONAL-SIM-SHORT-HOP-LATCH`, `PROVISIONAL-SIM-SHORT-HOP-HEIGHT`, `PROVISIONAL-LEDGE-GETUP-FRAMES`, `PROVISIONAL-CORNER-FORGIVENESS-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-SIM-LIP-POP`, `PROVISIONAL-MOVEMENT-ABILITY-GRAVITY-SCOPE`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-HAZARD-BLOCK-SOURCES`, `PROVISIONAL-STORY-BASIC-FLOOR-ORDER`, `PROVISIONAL-DEVICE-FOLLOW-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-ULTIMATE-CHORD-LENIENCY`, `PROVISIONAL-PAD-MENU-RULES`, `PROVISIONAL-REHIT-COUNTER-RULES` and `PROVISIONAL-BOT-FLIGHT-MODEL`; `VERIFY-WALL-PIN` (the pad session's row 2), `VERIFY-SIM-PIT-FLOOR-SNAP`, `VERIFY-AERIAL-SPECIAL-HANG-PARITY`, `VERIFY-STORY-DOUBLE-TAP-CHAIN-HOLD`, `VERIFY-SIM-JUMP-LATCH-EDGE-LOSS`, `VERIFY-NEAR-CAPTURE-PAUSE-OWNER`, `VERIFY-CPU-DELIVERY-RESIDUALS`, the widened `VERIFY-STORY-INVULN-LAYER-ORDER` (the hazard half is met; the hurtbox path still spends a block charge on an invulnerable hero, and the unblockable penalties still spend Aegis or the barrier), and `VERIFY-SAME-TICK-VERB-PARITY`'s new items (8), Story firing a Special's effect one tick before the sim, and (9), the sim's Ultimate on a Block-release tick. **Questions for the user:** whether the CPU should reach-check Splitting Strike or any melee Special before pressing it (`VERIFY-CPU-SPLITTING-STRIKE-REACH`; 9.3 left kit awareness out); whether jumping or attacking should page non-pausing dialogue at all, now that pad A (Jump) does as keyboard Space always did, and whether an Interact hold should arm hold-to-skip in a bark (`VERIFY-BARK-INPUT-RULES`); whether a defied hit should still cancel the survivor's own roll or air dodge in the sim (`VERIFY-DEFY-CANCELS-OWN-ROLL`); every 1-tick short-hop tap measures 41.6–41.9 % of the held apex, below the decided ~45 % (recorded, not tuned, under Q4: `PROVISIONAL-SIM-SHORT-HOP-HEIGHT`); whether the Hard Mirror clone should wear the gold hero aura (`VERIFY-MIRROR-CLONE-HERO-AURA`), and whether the telegraph, built steady, should flash as the ruling's "red flash" says (`USER-ULT-TELEGRAPH`); and the mercy-invulnerability ruling itself, which the after batch's `rehits` table now informs (`USER-STORY-MERCY-MEASURE-FIRST`: over 20 runs a side, 11 cross-source re-hits within 30 frames among 400 hits taken on Easy, 1 of them combat, and 31 among 542 on Normal, 25 of them combat; no same-frame doubles). Closed by the update: `DEFER-SIM-SPECIAL-PHASES`, `SIM-SPECIAL-POSE-THIRDS` (its thirds path kept for the fallback and the press-tick Ultimate edge), `DEFER-ULT-TELEGRAPH`, `VERIFY-STORY-HAZARD-BLOCK`, `VERIFY-SIM-STRING-FACING-PER-TICK`, `VERIFY-GAMEPAD-ULTIMATE-CHORD`, `VERIFY-SAME-TICK-VERB-PARITY` (1) and (5), the short-hop half of `DEFER-H7-STORY-TERMINAL`, M-18, and the rows written already closed for the review rounds' findings (among them `VERIFY-SIM-KNOCKDOWN-LANDING-TICK`, `VERIFY-SIM-A02-THAW-TICK`, `VERIFY-STORY-KIT-MOTION-FIRST-TICK`, `VERIFY-CPU-LEDGE-GETUPS`, `FIX-PAD-MENU-ACCEPT-CANCEL` and the four `FIX-CPU-*` rows).

Recorded limits worth knowing before a pad session: the CPU stays kit-blind (9.3) — a CPU Lincoln presses Splitting Strike unchecked (4 casts, 2 whiffs on the merged build in the Hard-vs-Hard smoke, `FighterCpuSpecialTimingSmokeTests`), and the CPU uses no deliberate D3, ledge get-up or perfect block, though a reactive Hard CPU's pulsed Block can perfect-block by accident (2 perfect blocks among 52 blocks in the merged build's smoke; its `[CM-CANCEL smoke]` lines print the per-stage counts); the Fighter has no new pose, flash or HUD cue for a D3 cancel; in the sim a Jump pressed during a pause and held through the resume keeps the ×2.5 cut for that airtime, and a mid-air device switch can drop a held Jump's release edge (`VERIFY-SIM-JUMP-LATCH-EDGE-LOSS`); a Story pause opened over Level 9's near-capture struggle still unpauses the world on Resume; the respawn glow can outlast the grant by a late roll or air dodge; nobody has checked headless whether pad B closes an open `OptionButton` popup, a pad-only player cannot cancel a pad-slot listen in Settings, a mouse user's first click on the Local Versus select only joins, the device poll does not run while the tree is paused, and a Fighter stage launched directly never begins a join; on LAN builds from before and after desync, and the Fighter wind-up cue, an edge presentation observes, can play twice or not at all across a rollback correction (LAN is de-scoped); the wind-up cue and the perfect-block flash are placeholders (the cue is silence); Level 7's quay lock stalls the Lincoln `play` bot on Normal, a bot gap the held hang exposed, not a content block (`PROVISIONAL-BOT-FLIGHT-MODEL`); and bot batches, CPU recovery numbers and Fighter hashes from before and after the update are not comparable (the flights moved, and a first-grab recovery now leaves with an ordinary vulnerable climb jump).

When a task closes one of these gaps, update this file so later agents inherit the new reality.
